# Named regions are the backbone for final-build level scripting.  Each zone has
# a tile-space rect and a generated world-space rect.  Clues, travel-buffer
# triggers, ambient events, and camera logic can all reference these names.
ZONE_DEFS = {
    "approach": {
        "label": "West Approach Road",
        "tile_rect": pygame.Rect(2, 66, 30, 12),
        "floor": ".",
        "color": (38, 37, 34),
    },

        "floor": "d",
        "color": (30, 35, 34),
    },

        "label": "North Service Road",
        "tile_rect": pygame.Rect(43, 36, 25, 20),
        "floor": "s",
        "color": (33, 36, 35),
    },
    "toll_plaza": {
        "label": "Checkpoint 12 Toll Plaza / Kill-Box",
        "tile_rect": pygame.Rect(18, 28, 39, 16),
        "floor": ".",
        "color": (34, 34, 33),
    },
    "east_control": {
        "label": "East Control Building / Upload Sector",
        "tile_rect": pygame.Rect(39, 7, 29, 23),
        "floor": "O",
        "color": (29, 36, 38),
    },
}


def _tc(col, row):
    return col * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2, row * TILE_SIZE + TILE_SIZE // 2


def _tile_rect(col, row, w=1, h=1):
    return pygame.Rect(col * TILE_SIZE, row * TILE_SIZE, w * TILE_SIZE, h * TILE_SIZE)


def _zone_world_rect(zone_name):
    tr = ZONE_DEFS[zone_name]["tile_rect"]
    return _tile_rect(tr.x, tr.y, tr.w, tr.h)


ZONE_WORLD_RECTS = {name: _zone_world_rect(name) for name in ZONE_DEFS}


def get_zone_for_world_pos(pos):
    """Return the authored zone name for a world-space point, or None."""
    for name, rect in ZONE_WORLD_RECTS.items():
        if rect.collidepoint(pos):
            return name
    return None

# ── Officers' leisure garden (behind the control building) ──────────────
# The control building's north wall (row 9) is solid — no door on the
# garden side. The garden is a decorated area the player can wander into
# by walking around the NW corner (col 50) or NE corner (col 68) of the
# building. It's an environmental storytelling beat: a manicured officers'
# garden behind a building used for framing MAG creates a contrast that
# makes the IPA feel more human and more monstrous at the same time.
#
# Layout (top-down, N=up, building south wall at row 9):
#   r1:  [tree]  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  [tree]
#   r2:  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers
#   r3:  [hedge NW]              path           [hedge NE]
#   r4:  [hedge NW]  [bench] [fountain] [bench] [hedge NE]
#   r5:  [hedge NW]              path           [hedge NE]
#   r6:  [hedge SW]              path           [hedge SE]
#   r7:  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers
#   r8:  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers  flowers
#         c51      c53      c55-63      c65      c67
#
# The 4 hedges (NW/NE/SW/SE quadrants) have collision — they force the
# player to use the central E-W path. Flowers, benches, and trees are
# walkable (low props). The fountain has collision (it's a solid stone
# basin). Lamp posts have collision (thin poles).
GARDEN_CENTER = _tc(59, 4)        # fountain centerpiece
GARDEN_PATH_NORTH_Y = _tc(0, 3)[1]  # y of path row 3
GARDEN_PATH_SOUTH_Y = _tc(0, 6)[1]  # y of path row 6
# Hedge quadrant rects (world-space pygame.Rects). Each is a 3x2-tile block
# leaving a 3-tile-wide path gap in the middle (cols 55-63).
_HEDGE_W = 3 * TILE_SIZE  # 3 tiles wide
_HEDGE_H = 3 * TILE_SIZE  # 3 tiles tall (rows 3-5 for N hedges, row 6 only would be too thin)
# North hedges: rows 3-5, cols 51-53 (NW) and 65-67 (NE)
# South hedges: row 6, cols 51-53 (SW) and 65-67 (SE) — shorter, just 1 tile
# Actually, let's make all 4 hedges the same size for symmetry: 3x2 tiles
# NW hedge: cols 51-53, rows 3-4
# NE hedge: cols 65-67, rows 3-4
# SW hedge: cols 51-53, rows 5-6
# SE hedge: cols 65-67, rows 5-6
# Path gap: cols 54-64 (11 tiles = 704px wide), rows 3-6 — plenty of room
GARDEN_HEDGE_RECTS = [
    pygame.Rect(51 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE, 2 * TILE_SIZE),  # NW
    pygame.Rect(65 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE, 2 * TILE_SIZE),  # NE
    pygame.Rect(51 * TILE_SIZE, 5 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE, 2 * TILE_SIZE),  # SW
    pygame.Rect(65 * TILE_SIZE, 5 * TILE_SIZE, 3 * TILE_SIZE, 2 * TILE_SIZE),  # SE
]
# Fountain collision (circular basin approximated as a rect, ~2 tiles wide)
GARDEN_FOUNTAIN_RECT = pygame.Rect(
    GARDEN_CENTER[0] - TILE_SIZE, GARDEN_CENTER[1] - TILE_SIZE,
    2 * TILE_SIZE, 2 * TILE_SIZE,
)
# Lamp post collision (thin poles flanking the fountain, on the path)
# NOTE: lamps are placed at cols 55 and 63 (just outside the fountain's
# 2-tile radius), NOT at the path entrances (cols 54/64). The hedges
# already gate the entrances — putting lamps there too would over-block.
# These lamps read as path lighting, framing the fountain from both sides.
GARDEN_LAMP_RECTS = [
    pygame.Rect(_tc(55, 3)[0] - 4, _tc(55, 3)[1] - 4, 8, 8),   # NW of fountain
    pygame.Rect(_tc(63, 5)[0] - 4, _tc(63, 5)[1] - 4, 8, 8),   # SE of fountain
]
# Ornamental trees (corners) — collision as small rects (trunk)
GARDEN_TREE_RECTS = [
    pygame.Rect(_tc(51, 1)[0] - 6, _tc(51, 1)[1] - 6, 12, 12),  # NW corner
    pygame.Rect(_tc(67, 1)[0] - 6, _tc(67, 1)[1] - 6, 12, 12),  # NE corner
    pygame.Rect(_tc(51, 8)[0] - 6, _tc(51, 8)[1] - 6, 12, 12),  # SW corner
    pygame.Rect(_tc(67, 8)[0] - 6, _tc(67, 8)[1] - 6, 12, 12),  # SE corner
]
# Benches (on the path, east and west of fountain) — collision as rect.
# Benches are 64x24 (a full tile wide, 3/8 tile deep) so they're clearly
# recognizable furniture, not tiny wood blocks. They sit perpendicular to
# the path: the west bench's backrest faces west (away from the fountain)
# so the seat faces the centerpiece; the east bench mirrors it.
GARDEN_BENCH_RECTS = [
    pygame.Rect(_tc(56, 4)[0] - 32, _tc(56, 4)[1] - 12, 64, 24),  # west bench (faces E toward fountain)
    pygame.Rect(_tc(62, 4)[0] - 32, _tc(62, 4)[1] - 12, 64, 24),  # east bench (faces W toward fountain)
]

# Post-ambush gameplay anchors.  These turn the cutscene's promise (the road is
# a sniper kill-box) into playable objectives: get cover, survive sightlines,
# then flank the gantry.
KILLBOX_COVER_POSITIONS = [
    _tc(34, 39), _tc(40, 40), _tc(30, 42), _tc(42, 34), _tc(28, 37)
]
KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS = [

    _tc(55, 45),  # north service route
]
SNIPER_FLANK_POS = _tc(54, 28)
KILLBOX_DANGER_RECT = _tile_rect(27, 30, 20, 15)
SNIPER_MAX_LOS_RANGE = 1900          # sniper sees far down Route 12
# Flank cutscene timing (in frames @ 60fps). Total = 540 = 9 seconds.
# Pacing is deliberately slow on the tension beats (push-in, hold+aim,
# stamp hold) and fast on the reaction beats (startle, scramble).
FLANK_CUTSCENE_DURATION     = 540
FLANK_BEAT_PUSH_IN_END      = 90    # 1.5s — slow dolly-in completes
FLANK_BEAT_STARTLE          = 180   # 3.0s — sniper spots the flanker
FLANK_BEAT_SCRAMBLE_BEGIN   = 220   # 3.66s — silhouette drops rifle, crawls away
FLANK_BEAT_BURST            = 300   # 5.0s — big dust + debris + casing scatter
FLANK_BEAT_STAMP_FADE_IN    = 330   # 5.5s — "NEST ABANDONED" stamp begins appearing
FLANK_BEAT_STAMP_FULL       = 390   # 6.5s — stamp fully visible
FLANK_BEAT_LETTERBOX_OUT    = 510   # 8.5s — letterbox bars start retracting
# Unified close-range radius. The old code had two thresholds
# (SNIPER_POINT_BLANK_RANGE = 520 and SNIPER_CLOSE_KILL_RADIUS = 170) which
# produced confusing overlap. 170px = walking right under the gantry.
SNIPER_POINT_BLANK_RANGE = 170
SNIPER_AIM_FRAMES = 34               # normal exposed aim time
SNIPER_CLOSE_AIM_FRAMES = 8          # close-range snap shot
SNIPER_DAMAGE = 75                   # normal sniper hit
SNIPER_EXECUTION_DAMAGE = 9999       # too close = dead
REGROUP_ENTRY_POSITIONS = [
    _tc(33, 38),   # Gabi — clear path north
    _tc(35, 38),   # Seby
    _tc(37, 38),   # Darius
]
REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS = [

    _tc(35, 35),   # Seby center
    _tc(37, 35),   # Darius right
]

# Post-cutscene sniper pressure tuning.
SNIPER_GRACE_FRAMES = 90        # time after cutscene before sniper starts lethal tracking
SNIPER_LOCK_FRAMES = 105        # exposed frames before instant kill
SNIPER_LOCK_DECAY = 5           # how quickly lock drains in cover
SNIPER_WARN_THRESHOLDS = (35, 65, 90)

# Reinforcement spawn anchors for the kill-box.  These are perimeter positions
# (service-road gate, ditch culvert, toll-booth alleys, gantry support) where
# IPA shooters can plausibly pour in once the player commits to cover.  Each
# is a world-space anchor; spawns are placed near these with small jitter.
KILLBOX_REINFORCEMENT_SPAWNS = [
    (58, 32),  # north service road, east of gantry
    (54, 41),  # north service road, behind toll booths
    (46, 28),  # gantry support, drops onto road
    (20, 41),  # south ditch culvert, west flank
    (26, 44),  # south ditch, closer push
    (50, 34),  # toll booth alley, mid-right
    (30, 30),  # toll booth alley, mid-left
]

# Flanker spawns — INSIDE the control building, behind where the player
# typically camps during the evidence phase. Used by waves 2 and 3 so the
# player can't just hold one doorway; they have to watch their back.
# Positions are interior tiles with validated wall clearance (the spawn
# function tries 5 nearby positions if the first is blocked).
KILLBOX_FLANK_SPAWNS = [
    (55, 13),  # NW control building interior — behind player entering from south
    (63, 14),  # NE control building interior — flanks the upload terminal approach
    (58, 20),  # SW control building interior — cuts off retreat to the tanker area
]

            {"kind": "flare", "pos": _tc(30, 61)},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "ditch_static",
        "zone": "south_ditch",
        "rect": _tile_rect(6, 47, 17, 10),
        "dialogue": 'DARIUS: "Static is pooling down here. Something jammed the channel."',
        "sound": "radio_static",
        "volume": 0.28,
        "props": [
            {"kind": "broken_camera", "pos": _tc(17, 52)},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "service_gate",
        "zone": "north_service",
        "rect": _tile_rect(48, 41, 17, 9),
        "dialogue": 'VASILE: "Government tread, checkpoint equipment... keep your heads down."',
        "sound": "menu_hover",
        "volume": 0.14,
        "props": [
            {"kind": "spark_box", "pos": _tc(61, 43)},
        ],
    },
    {
        "id": "gantry_glint_preview",
        "zone": "toll_plaza",
        "rect": _tile_rect(28, 31, 18, 8),
        "dialogue": 'SEBY: "Did anyone else see that flash above the gantry?"',
        "sound": "menu_hover",
        "volume": 0.18,
        "props": [
            {"kind": "glint", "pos": SNIPER_NEST_POS},
        ],
    },
]


# Reordered cutscene per the 12-point rework.
#
# - "wrong_scan": squad looks the WRONG way (west cars / south ditch).  This
#   widens the gap between what the audience knows and what the squad knows.
# - "gabi_shot" is now SHORT and has NO laser telegraph for the first kill —
#   she dies mid-sentence to maximize shock.
# - "dead_air" is a held silent beat after Gabi falls (no music, ducked ambience,
#   tinnitus, dripping radio).  The squad is frozen in disbelief.
# - "origin_reveal" now happens AFTER the first death: muzzle flash, brief
#   silhouette of the shooter on the gantry, then he hides again.
# - Seby's death is long and physical (he drags Gabi, shouts at the gantry).
# - Darius's death is mid-radio-call: he reaches for the field radio handset
#   and dies whispering a mayday into it.
# - "biribel_near_miss" freezes player input, slams camera to impact, etc.
# - "handoff": single hard black frame at the very end before gameplay.
SNIPER_PHASES = [
    {"name": "regroup",        "duration": 100},
    {"name": "movement_one",   "duration": 720},
    {"name": "movement_two",   "duration": 600},
    {"name": "movement_three", "duration": 600},
]

SNIPER_BEAT_CLOCK = {
    "regroup": {
        "spread_line": 80,
    },
    "movement_one": {
        "regroup_line":                70,
        "scan_west":                  170,
        "gabi_crack":                 300,
        "tried_to_save_window_start": 320,
        "tried_to_save_window_end":   360,
        "silence_start":              302,
        "silence_frames":             110,
        "vasile_down":                320,
        "origin_flash":               440,
        "muzzle_flash":               440,
        "gantry_hold":                460,
        "seby_react":                 660,
    },
    "movement_two": {
        "seby_breaks":             70,
        "seby_arrives":            190,
        "seby_drag_to_cover_end":  460,
        "seby_crack":              430,
        "vasile_cover":            100,
        "vasile_hold":             450,
        "darius_reaches":          500,
        "darius_crack":            570,
    },
    "movement_three": {
        "near_miss": 110,
        "weapon_pickup": 180,
        "hard_black": 320,
    },
}


def _beat(phase, event, default=0):
    return SNIPER_BEAT_CLOCK.get(phase, {}).get(event, default)

def _beat_now(phase, pt, event):
    f = SNIPER_BEAT_CLOCK.get(phase, {}).get(event)
    return f is not None and pt == f


def _set_tile(g, col, row, tile):
    if 0 <= col < MAP_W and 0 <= row < MAP_H:
        g[row][col] = tile


def _paint_tiles(g, rect, tile):
    for row in range(rect.y, rect.y + rect.h):
        for col in range(rect.x, rect.x + rect.w):
            _set_tile(g, col, row, tile)


def _outline_building(g, col, row, w, h, interior="O", door_tiles=()):
    for yy in range(row, row + h):
        for xx in range(col, col + w):
            border = xx in (col, col + w - 1) or yy in (row, row + h - 1)
            _set_tile(g, xx, yy, "C" if border else interior)
    for dx, dy in door_tiles:
        _set_tile(g, col + dx, row + dy, interior)


def _generate_map():
    g = [["g" for _ in range(MAP_W)] for _ in range(MAP_H)]

    # Paint authored regions first.  Collidable structure is added after.
    for zone in ZONE_DEFS.values():
        _paint_tiles(g, zone["tile_rect"], zone["floor"])

    # Continuous highway spine and shoulders.  These broad open sightlines are
    # intentional: the sniper ambush later weaponizes this road geometry.
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(17, 23, 37, 55), ".")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(12, 54, 42, 13), ".")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(7, 70, 25, 8), ".")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(4, 47, 23, 15), "d")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(45, 38, 23, 15), "s")
    _paint_tiles(g, pygame.Rect(39, 12, 28, 16), "O")

    # World border.
    for x in range(MAP_W):
        g[0][x] = g[MAP_H - 1][x] = "C"
    for y in range(MAP_H):
        g[y][0] = g[y][MAP_W - 1] = "C"

    # Control building and offices.
    _outline_building(g, 51, 9, 17, 17, interior="O", door_tiles=((6, 16), (7, 16), (0, 9)))
    # Smaller security hut / checkpoint utility room.
    _outline_building(g, 41, 18, 9, 8, interior="O", door_tiles=((4, 7),))

    # Toll booths and gantry supports.
    for bx, by in [(23, 32), (30, 32), (44, 32), (51, 32), (24, 37), (45, 37)]:
        for yy in range(by, by + 2):
            for xx in range(bx, bx + 2):
                _set_tile(g, xx, yy, "C")
    for sx, sy in [(56, 25), (59, 25), (56, 27), (59, 27)]:
        _set_tile(g, sx, sy, "C")

    # Barriers / cover.  These break up travel without deleting the long sniper
    # sightline through the center lane.
    barriers = [
        (12, 72), (13, 72), (21, 69), (22, 69),
        (18, 61), (19, 61), (27, 60), (28, 60), (37, 58),
        (10, 51), (11, 51), (20, 47), (21, 47),
        (29, 40), (30, 40), (34, 39), (35, 39), (40, 40),
        (47, 45), (48, 45), (62, 47), (63, 47),
        (44, 27), (45, 27), (49, 28), (50, 28),
        (57, 21), (58, 21), (62, 18), (63, 18),
    ]
    for col, row in barriers:
        _set_tile(g, col, row, "B")

    # Final-build density pass: guardrails, broken dividers, service-road clutter,
    # and ditch obstructions.  These are intentionally staggered so the map has
    # shape without blocking the central sniper sightline.
    for row in range(25, 76, 4):
        if row not in (32, 33, 34, 35, 36, 37, 38, 39):
            _set_tile(g, 16, row, "B")
            _set_tile(g, 54, row + (row // 4) % 2, "B")
    for row in range(55, 66, 3):
        for col in (14, 41, 46, 51):
            _set_tile(g, col, row, "B")
    for col in range(6, 26, 4):
        _set_tile(g, col, 46, "B")
        _set_tile(g, col + 1, 59, "B")
    for col in range(46, 66, 4):
        _set_tile(g, col, 40, "B")
        _set_tile(g, col + 1, 51, "B")
    for col in range(21, 53, 7):
        _set_tile(g, col, 30, "B")
        _set_tile(g, col + 2, 42, "B")
    # Interior partitions in the control building so it no longer reads as one
    # empty rectangle.  Door gaps keep all evidence reachable.
    for col in range(53, 67):
        if col not in (57, 62):
            _set_tile(g, col, 17, "C")
    for row in range(11, 25):
        if row not in (15, 21):
            _set_tile(g, 60, row, "C")

    # Tanker footprint.  The actual collision uses mission2_tanker_rect.
    for row in range(36, 39):
        for col in range(43, 48):
            _set_tile(g, col, row, "T")

    _set_tile(g, 8, 74, "P")
    _set_tile(g, 64, 12, "X")
    return ["".join(row) for row in g]


MISSION2_MAP = _generate_map()

# Module-level draw/update scratch.
_last_keys = None
_objective = "Inspect staged evidence markers with [E]"
_radio_marks = set()

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Utility helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _dist(a, b):
    return math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1])


def _player_pos():
    if not STATE.player:
        return (0, 0)
    return STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery


def _near(pos, radius=80):
    return STATE.player and _dist(_player_pos(), pos) <= radius


def _camera_offset_for_target(target):
    tx, ty = target
    max_x = max(0, MAP_W * TILE_SIZE - WIDTH)
    max_y = max(0, MAP_H * TILE_SIZE - HEIGHT)
    cam_x = tx - WIDTH // 2
    cam_y = ty - HEIGHT // 2
    cam_x = max(0, min(max_x, cam_x))
    cam_y = max(0, min(max_y, cam_y))
    return cam_x, cam_y


def _snap_camera_to(target=None):
    if target is None:
        target = _player_pos()
    cam_x, cam_y = _camera_offset_for_target(target)
    STATE.camera_x = cam_x
    STATE.camera_y = cam_y
    STATE.cam_kick_x = 0.0
    STATE.cam_kick_y = 0.0
    STATE.screen_shake = 0


def _solid_walls():
    return (
        list(STATE.walls)
        + list(getattr(STATE, "mission2_vehicle_walls", []))
        + list(getattr(STATE, "mission2_prop_walls", []))
        + list(getattr(STATE, "mission2_shelter_walls", []))
        + list(getattr(STATE, "mission2_garden_walls", []))
    )


def _build_shelter_walls():
    """Build collision rects for the shelter's crescent blast wall.

    The blast wall is drawn (in _draw_shelter) as a filled polygon following
    an arc from N (0°) to E (90°) on the NE side of SAFE_ZONE, with outer
    radius 66 and inner radius 50 (16px thick). This function approximates
    that crescent with a series of small axis-aligned rectangles — one per
    arc segment — so the player and enemies collide with the wall and
    cannot walk through it (or off the pad on the NE side, which would
    defeat the shelter's purpose during the tanker blast).

    Each segment rect spans the angular slice [a, a+step] and the radial
    range [inner_r, outer_r]. The rect is the axis-aligned bounding box of
    that slice's 4 corners — slightly conservative (the AABB is a bit larger
    than the actual arc slice), but that's the right direction for a wall:
    better to block a touch too much than let the player clip through.

    The rects are stored on STATE.mission2_shelter_walls and included in
    _solid_walls() above. Call this once from start_mission2() after
    STATE.reset() has cleared the list; the dev-args shortcuts
    (--m2-truck, --m2-flank, etc.) inherit it via start_mission2() so they
    don't need their own call.
    """
    sx, sy = SAFE_ZONE
    wall_outer_r = 66   # MUST match _draw_shelter's wall_outer_r
    wall_inner_r = 50   # MUST match _draw_shelter's wall_inner_r
    # Number of arc segments. More = smoother collision, more rects.
    # 12 segments = 7.5° per segment — feels solid without bloating the
    # wall list (the player rect is 32x32, so 7.5° slices are well below
    # the player's footprint and there are no gap-through exploits).
    num_segments = 12
    walls = []
    for i in range(num_segments):
        a0 = math.radians(90.0 * i / num_segments)        # start angle
        a1 = math.radians(90.0 * (i + 1) / num_segments)  # end angle
        # 4 corners of this arc slice (outer0, outer1, inner1, inner0)
        # Screen coords: x = cos(angle) * r, y = -sin(angle) * r
        # (negative sin because screen y goes down but "north" is up)
        corners = [
            (sx + math.cos(a0) * wall_outer_r, sy - math.sin(a0) * wall_outer_r),
            (sx + math.cos(a1) * wall_outer_r, sy - math.sin(a1) * wall_outer_r),
            (sx + math.cos(a1) * wall_inner_r, sy - math.sin(a1) * wall_inner_r),
            (sx + math.cos(a0) * wall_inner_r, sy - math.sin(a0) * wall_inner_r),
        ]
        xs = [c[0] for c in corners]
        ys = [c[1] for c in corners]
        rx = int(min(xs))
        ry = int(min(ys))
        rw = int(max(xs) - min(xs)) + 1  # +1 to avoid 1px gaps between segments
        rh = int(max(ys) - min(ys)) + 1
        walls.append(pygame.Rect(rx, ry, rw, rh))
    STATE.mission2_shelter_walls = walls


def _set_objective(text):
    global _objective
    _objective = text


def _radio(text, color=C_VASILE, duration=300, mark=None, allow_when_cut=False):
    """Small radio helper. Used sparingly; cards do most story work."""
    if mark and mark in _radio_marks:
        return
    if mark:
        _radio_marks.add(mark)

    # Sniper subtitles need reading time (~4 seconds minimum).
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER:
        duration = max(duration, 240)

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_comms_cut", False) and not allow_when_cut:
        text = "RADIO: <static>"
        color = (125, 125, 135)
        duration = min(duration, 140)
    # Muffled radio after the near-miss: drop letters, insert ellipses, fade
    # color so it visually reads as "underwater" without needing a real
    # low-pass filter.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0) > 0 and not text.startswith("RADIO:"):
        text = _muffle_radio_text(text)
        color = tuple(max(70, int(c * 0.62)) for c in color)
        if getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") == STAGE_SNIPER:
            duration = max(duration, 240)
        else:
            duration = max(160, int(duration * 0.75))
    trigger_radio_chatter(text, color=color, duration=duration)


def _muffle_radio_text(text):
    """Drop syllables and insert ellipses so a radio line reads as ear-ringing.

    Ramps the muffle severity based on mission2_radio_muffle_timer so the
    effect fades out gradually as the player's hearing returns.
    """
    timer = getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0)
    if timer <= 0:
        return text
    if ":" in text:
        speaker, _, spoken = text.partition(":")
        spoken = spoken.strip().strip('"')
        words = spoken.split()
        if len(words) <= 2:
            return text
        intensity = min(0.6, timer / 180.0)
        keep = []
        for i, w in enumerate(words):
            if i == 0 or random.random() > intensity:
                keep.append(w)
        return f'{speaker}: "{" . . . ".join(keep)}"'
    return text


# _burst is now an alias for the shared effects.burst. Both missions use the
# same particle-burst parameters (life 18-50, jitter ±8, speed_mul scaling,
# respects SETTINGS.particle_density) so blood / sparks / debris look the
# same across the campaign.
_burst = effects.burst


def _open_card(title, lines, color=C_TERMINAL, after=None, kind="document"):
    """Open a readable inspection/intel card. It stays until the player presses E."""
    if isinstance(lines, str):
        lines = [lines]
    STATE.mission2_card = {
        "title": title,
        "lines": list(lines),
        "color": color,
        "age": 0,
        "after": after,
        "kind": kind,
    }
    audio.play("terminal_glitch", volume=0.24)


def _close_card():
    card = getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None)
    if not card:
        return
    after = card.get("after")
    STATE.mission2_card = None
    audio.play("menu_click", volume=0.24)
    if after == "truck_done":
        STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_TANKER
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_tanker"
        _set_objective("Approach the fuel tanker — inspect with [E]")
        _radio('VASILE: "Balaceanu logistics. Not MAG. Move to the tanker — slow. Confirm what we\'re dealing with."',
               color=(255, 170, 80), mark="truck_done")
    elif after == "tanker_inspected":
        _radio('VASILE: "Biribel, get back! That\'s a remote-detonated cleanup charge — same frequency as the sniper. They\'re going to blow it!"',
               color=(255, 110, 70), mark="tanker_trap_revealed")
    elif after == "blast_done":
        STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_evidence"
        # ── Checkpoint reached (belt-and-suspenders re-set) ──
        # The checkpoint flag is now set inside _explode_tanker() itself, so
        # it's active the moment the tanker blows — regardless of whether
        # the player survives the blast. This re-set here is idempotent and
        # kept as a safety net in case some future code path reaches
        # _explode_tanker() without setting the flag (e.g. a dev-args shortcut
        # that skips the explosion and jumps straight to the card). The
        # canonical set is in _explode_tanker(); see the comment there for
        # the full rationale.
        # The evidence phase is a long, difficult fight (16 enemies across
        # 3 waves + persistent fire zones) — forcing a full mission restart
        # on death would be punishing in a way that feels unfair, not
        # challenging.
        STATE.mission2_checkpoint_reached = True
        # Release the cinematic blast-camera lock so the camera returns to
        # the player. Without this, mission2_camera_focus stays pinned to
        # TANKER_POS forever (set in _explode_tanker, never auto-cleared),
        # so the camera stays glued to the blast site for the entire rest
        # of the mission. Mirrors the flank-cutscene fix at the bottom of
        # _update_flank_cutscene.
        STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
        STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
        _set_objective("Enter the control building and inspect green evidence terminals")
        _spawn_control_cleanup()
    elif after == "evidence_ready":
        # ── Completion cinematic (Option E) ──
        # When the 3rd evidence item is read, trigger a 5-second cinematic
        # before the upload objective appears. The cinematic has 4 shots:
        #   1. White flash (camera flash) — 0.3s
        #   2. Evidence wall zoom — 1.5s
        #   3. Vasile radio line — 2.0s
        #   4. Upload terminal punch-in — 1.2s
        # The cinematic is driven in _update_evidence_cinematic() and drawn
        # in _draw_evidence_cinematic().
        STATE.mission2_evidence_cinematic_active = True
        STATE.mission2_evidence_cinematic_timer = 0
        STATE.mission2_evidence_cinematic_shot = 0
        STATE.mission2_evidence_cinematic_flash = 255  # full white at start
    elif after == "upload_done":
        STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EXFIL
        STATE.mission_phase = "m2_exfil"
        STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
        _set_objective("Reach the green exfil marker")
        _radio('SYSTEM: Evidence transmitted. Move to extraction.', color=C_TERMINAL, mark="exfil", allow_when_cut=True)


def _spawn_enemy(col, row, hp=115, speed=2.0, sniper=False):
    """Spawn a Mission 2 IPA grunt (or the kill-box sniper marker).

    The sniper enemy is a facing/HP marker only — the kill-box pressure
    system in _update_sniper_pressure drives actual fire. Speed and HP
    are forced for the sniper regardless of caller args because the
    sniper is an authored fixed-position actor.
    """
    x, y = _tc(col, row)
    e = Enemy(x - 16, y - 16)
    if sniper:
        e.hp = 90
    else:
        e.hp = hp
        e.speed = speed
    e.alerted = True
    e.state = "CHASE"
    e.last_seen_pos = _player_pos()
    e.fire_cooldown = random.randint(18, 75)
    e.is_mission2_ipa = True
    e.is_mission2_sniper = sniper
    STATE.enemies.append(e)
    return e


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Initialization
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _build_world():
    STATE.walls = []
    STATE.doors = []
    STATE.enemies = []
    STATE.pickups = []
    STATE.blood_decals = []
    STATE.bullets = []
    STATE.casings = []
    STATE.particles = []
    STATE.thrown_bottles = []
    STATE.molotov_fires = []
    STATE.fire_zones = []
    STATE.floating_texts = []
    STATE.mission2_barriers = []
    STATE.mission2_vehicle_walls = []
    STATE.mission2_prop_walls = []
    # Shelter blast-wall collision rects. Built by _build_shelter_walls()
    # below — a series of small rects approximating the crescent blast wall
    # on the NE side of SAFE_ZONE. Included in _solid_walls() so the player
    # and enemies cannot walk through the wall. Reset to empty here so a
    # mission restart doesn't accumulate stale rects from a previous run.
    STATE.mission2_shelter_walls = []
    # Garden collision rects (hedges, fountain, lamps, trees, benches).
    # Built by _build_garden_walls() — a fixed set of rects that never
    # changes during play, so we could inline them, but keeping them on
    # STATE for symmetry with the other wall lists and so a future
    # dynamic garden feature (e.g. destroyable hedges) has a place to
    # mutate state.
    STATE.mission2_garden_walls = []
    STATE.player = None

    for row_idx, row in enumerate(MISSION2_MAP):
        for col_idx, tile in enumerate(row):
            x, y = col_idx * TILE_SIZE, row_idx * TILE_SIZE
            if tile == "C":
                STATE.walls.append(pygame.Rect(x, y, TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE))
            elif tile == "B":
                r = pygame.Rect(x + 4, y + 12, TILE_SIZE - 8, TILE_SIZE - 24)
                STATE.walls.append(r)
                STATE.mission2_barriers.append(r)
            elif tile == "P":
                STATE.player = Player(x + 16, y + 16, max_hp=115)

    if STATE.player is None:
        STATE.player = Player(PLAYER_START[0] - 16, PLAYER_START[1] - 16, max_hp=115)
    # Player speed is set every frame by _update_player based on stage,
    # so we don't need to set it here.  Leaving a value here was dead —
    # it was overwritten on the first frame anyway.

    STATE.mission2_tanker_rect = pygame.Rect(43 * TILE_SIZE + 6, 36 * TILE_SIZE + 8, TILE_SIZE * 5 - 12, TILE_SIZE * 3 - 16)
    STATE.walls.append(STATE.mission2_tanker_rect)

    # Build the shelter blast-wall collision rects. Must happen AFTER
    # STATE.reset() (which clears mission2_shelter_walls) and after the
    # tilemap walls are appended. The dev-args shortcuts (--m2-truck,
    # --m2-flank, --m2-checkpoint, --m2-evidence) all call start_mission2()
    # first, so they inherit the shelter walls automatically.
    _build_shelter_walls()
    # Build the garden collision rects (hedges, fountain, lamps, trees,
    # benches behind the control building). Same inheritance note as above.
    _build_garden_walls()

    # NOTE: No gantry wall. The gantry is purely visual — it shows the
    # crossbeam and catwalk where the sniper sits. Earlier versions added
    # collision under/around it, but that blocked the sniper's downward
    # line of sight to the road. Walking under the gantry IS dangerous:
    # the sniper fires straight down and the kill-box pressure system
    # reads LOS from SNIPER_NEST_POS to the player. Without the wall,
    # the sniper can actually shoot at you.

    # Vehicles are visual props and collision/LOS blockers, but are not drawn as
    # wall tiles.  Vehicle collision is returned by _solid_walls().
    # All cars start INTACT. The Route 12 bombing scene is staged
    # (planted evidence, no real blast damage — see Gabi's "blast
    # pattern is too clean" line), so having pre-burned cars would
    # contradict the narrative. The ONLY source of fire damage on this
    # map is the tanker explosion later in the mission, which chars
    # nearby civilian cars via _explode_tanker.
    STATE.mission2_vehicles = [
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(9 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 70 * TILE_SIZE + 14, 118, 44)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(18 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 60 * TILE_SIZE + 10, 126, 48)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(31 * TILE_SIZE + 5, 57 * TILE_SIZE + 16, 110, 42)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(12 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 48 * TILE_SIZE + 14, 112, 44)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(49 * TILE_SIZE + 5, 45 * TILE_SIZE + 12, 116, 44)},
        {"kind": "ipa_truck", "rect": pygame.Rect(46 * TILE_SIZE + 8, 31 * TILE_SIZE + 12, 160, 54)},
        {"kind": "civilian", "rect": pygame.Rect(41 * TILE_SIZE + 6, 25 * TILE_SIZE + 12, 126, 46)},
    ]
    for v in STATE.mission2_vehicles:
        # Initialize per-vehicle burn state. A car starts burning when a
        # molotov fire overlaps it; after the burn completes it becomes
        # a permanent "burned" wreck. See _update_molotovs + _draw_vehicle.
        v["burn_progress"] = 0.0   # 0.0 = intact, 1.0 = fully burned
        v["burning"] = False
        STATE.mission2_vehicle_walls.append(v["rect"].inflate(-12, -10))

    STATE.pickups.extend([
        [12 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 68 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "bandage"],
        [24 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 57 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "mag"],
        [54 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 43 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "bandage"],
        [57 * TILE_SIZE + 32, 24 * TILE_SIZE + 32, "mag"],
    ])


def _spawn_mission2_clues():
    """Create mutable clue state from the static clue definitions."""
    STATE.mission2_clues = []
    STATE.mission2_clues_found = set()
    for definition in MISSION2_CLUE_DEFS:
        clue = dict(definition)
        clue["done"] = False
        clue["world_zone"] = definition.get("zone")
        STATE.mission2_clues.append(clue)


def _setup_travel_triggers():
    """Build one-shot environmental story triggers for the larger map."""
    STATE.mission2_triggers = []
    STATE.mission2_story_props = []
    for definition in MISSION2_TRIGGER_DEFS:
        trig = dict(definition)
        trig["triggered"] = False
        STATE.mission2_triggers.append(trig)


# Only props that visually imply mass should block movement/line-of-sight.  Flares,
# soot, puddles, and glints stay decorative; cones/barrels/signs/electrical boxes
# get compact collision so they feel grounded without making navigation annoying.
STORY_PROP_COLLISION_SIZES = {
    "cone": (22, 18),
    "barrel": (30, 30),
    "sign": (34, 18),
    "spark_box": (30, 32),
    "field_radio": (24, 18),
    "broken_camera": (28, 18),
}


def _story_prop_collision_rect(prop):
    size = STORY_PROP_COLLISION_SIZES.get(prop.get("kind"))
    if not size:
        return None
    x, y = prop.get("pos", (0, 0))
    w, h = size
    return pygame.Rect(int(x - w // 2), int(y - h // 2), w, h)


def _sync_story_prop_collisions():
    STATE.mission2_prop_walls = []
    for prop in getattr(STATE, "mission2_story_props", []):
        rect = _story_prop_collision_rect(prop)
        if rect is not None:
            STATE.mission2_prop_walls.append(rect)

    _spawn_mission2_clues()
    _setup_travel_triggers()
    # Baseline non-interactive set dressing.  Trigger props still add more
    # moment-to-moment story beats, but these make the larger map feel occupied
    # immediately instead of waiting for trigger activation.

    ]:
        STATE.mission2_story_props.append({"kind": kind, "pos": _tc(col, row), "life": -1, "age": 0})
    _sync_story_prop_collisions()

    STATE.mission2_truck_manifest = {

def start_mission2(preserve_timer=False):
    """Called by game_core/acaristan to begin Mission 2 gameplay."""
    global _objective, _radio_marks, _last_keys
    STATE.reset()
    STATE.active_mission = MISSION2_KEY
    STATE.active_mission_id = MISSION2_ID
    STATE.active_mission_title = MISSION2_TITLE
    STATE.active_mission_operation = "ROUTE 12 — QUIET ON ROUTE 12"
    STATE.active_mission_outcome = "KRAP framed  ·  Evidence transmitted  ·  Comms compromised"
    STATE.active_mission_quote_attr = "Vasile, over broken static:"
    STATE.active_mission_quote = '"No. They framed you first."'
    STATE.show_minimap = True  # was False — caused players to think the minimap was broken in M2
    STATE.minimap = MiniMap(MISSION2_MAP)
    STATE.mission_start_time = STATE.mission_start_time if (preserve_timer and STATE.mission_start_time) else pygame.time.get_ticks()
    STATE.mission_end_time = 0
    STATE.hostiles_killed = 0
    STATE.shots_fired = 0
    STATE.shots_hit = 0
    STATE.request_mission_complete = False
    STATE.request_mission2_ending = False
    STATE.request_death_screen = False
    STATE.player_dead = False
    STATE.death_timer = 0

    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_INVESTIGATE
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_investigate"
    STATE.mission2_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_events = set()
    STATE.mission2_card = None
    STATE.mission2_comms_cut = False
    STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded = False
    STATE.mission2_countdown = 0
    STATE.mission2_upload_progress = 0.0
    STATE.mission2_upload_started = False
    STATE.mission2_evidence_uploaded = False
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_traces = []
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lasers = []
    STATE.mission2_abandoned_nest = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes = []
    STATE.mission2_sniper_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_near_miss_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_tinnitus_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_tanker_blast_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_tanker_shockwave_radius = 0
    STATE.mission2_tanker_shake_decay = 0
    # Aftermath visuals — initialized empty so drawing code can safely
    # getattr them before the blast fires. Populated in _explode_tanker.
    STATE.mission2_tanker_scorch = None
    STATE.mission2_tanker_scorch_cracks = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_destroyed = False
    STATE.mission2_tanker_debris_field = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_outer_debris = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_blast_streaks = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_asphalt_cracks = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_spot_fires = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_wreckage_ribs = []
    STATE.mission2_all_clues_found = False
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_current_zone = "approach"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = ""
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_grace_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_target = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_suppressed = False
    # Cleanup-team spawn gate for the post-flank "done" phase. Reset on
    # mission restart so the squad can spawn again on a fresh playthrough.
    STATE.mission2_cleanup_team_spawned = False
    # Tanker inspection gate. The player must press [E] at the tanker to
    # discover the hidden detonator before the run-to-shelter phase begins.
    # Resets on mission restart.
    STATE.mission2_tanker_inspected = False
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lock = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lock_max = SNIPER_LOCK_FRAMES
    STATE.mission2_sniper_warned = set()
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lethal_target = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_kill_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_close_execute_done = False
    STATE.mission2_cover_hold = 0
    STATE.mission2_reinforcement_waves = 0
    STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_reinforcement_triggered = False
    # Evidence-phase multi-wave reinforcement state. Reset on mission start
    # so a restart doesn't carry stale wave counters from a previous run.
    STATE.mission2_evidence_wave = 0
    STATE.mission2_evidence_wave_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_evidence_wave_cleared = False
    # Sniper-cutscene removed. Minimal combat state kept below.
    STATE.mission2_input_freeze = 0
    STATE.mission2_radio_muffle_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_heartbeat_active = False
    STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer = 0
    # One-shot radio gate — must be reset on mission start so the
    # "tanker manifest — second cargo" line can fire on a fresh playthrough.
    # Previously stayed True across restarts, silencing the line forever.
    STATE.mission2_late_reveal_played = False
    STATE.mission2_rain_suppressed = False
    STATE.mission2_rain_intensity = 1.0
    STATE.mission2_hard_black = 0
    STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_death_callout = None
    STATE.mission2_rain_drops = [[random.randint(0, WIDTH), random.randint(-80, HEIGHT), random.randint(10, 24)] for _ in range(180)]
    # Player try-to-save-Gabi state. Both default False so the
    # acknowledgment line in _m1_regroup_wrong_scan only fires if the
    # player actually pressed E during the attempt window.
    STATE.mission2_tried_to_save = False
    STATE.mission2_tried_to_save_acknowledged = False
    # Sniper cutscene phase state. These were previously only initialized
    # inside _start_sniper_sequence, which meant stale state could leak
    # between cutscene runs and silently change the next opening beat.
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase = "regroup"
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_events_fired = set()
    STATE.mission2_sniper_origin_revealed = False
    STATE.mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_camera_snap = None

    STATE.mission2_weapon_pickup_active = False
    STATE.mission2_weapon_pickup_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_weapon_pickup_ready = False
    STATE.mission2_fade_out = 0
    STATE.mission2_os12_acquired = False
    STATE.request_seby_pickup_cutscene = False
    STATE._m3_pickup_done = False
    STATE._seby_os12_granted = False
    STATE._m3_killbox_started = False
    STATE.player_locked = False

    STATE.os12_unlocked = False
    STATE.os12_ammo_in_mag = 25
    STATE.os12_spare_mags = 2
    STATE.os12_shot_cooldown = 0
    STATE.current_weapon = "pistol"

    STATE.ammo_in_mag = 12
    STATE.mag_size = 12
    STATE.spare_mags = 4
    STATE.player_bottles = 2
    STATE.shot_cooldown = 0
    STATE.is_reloading = False
    STATE.reload_timer = 0
    STATE.melee_active = False
    STATE.melee_timer = 0

    _build_world()
    _init_scripted_props()

    _snap_camera_to(_player_pos())
    STATE.mission2_camera_initialized = True  # legacy flag, kept for save compatibility
    STATE.camera_initialized = True  # shared flag: camera is already snapped, don't re-snap in update_camera

    _objective = f"Inspect staged evidence markers with [E] (0/{REQUIRED_INVESTIGATION_CLUES})"
    _radio_marks = set()
    _last_keys = None
    # Skip the investigation-phase intro radio line when starting from a
    # checkpoint or dev jump — it's wrong context for those entry points.
    if not getattr(STATE, "_skip_mission2_intro_radio", False):
        _radio('VASILE: "Start with the obvious lie. Inspect the staged evidence."', mark="start")
    else:
        STATE._skip_mission2_intro_radio = False  # reset the flag
    return STATE.player


def _mark_squad_dead():
    """Mark all KRAP squad actors as dead at their kill-box final positions.

    Used by all post-kill-box skip shortcuts (flank, truck, checkpoint,
    evidence-debug) to ensure the squad renders as corpses after the sniper
    kill-box has killed them. Without this, the squad would visibly be
    standing and alive at the regroup point despite the narrative having
    moved past their deaths.

    Positions match REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS (where they died during
    movement_one / movement_two) and the state matches what _kill_actor()
    sets: alive=False, activity="down".

    Note: start_mission2_sniper and start_mission2_after_sniper do NOT call
    this — those shortcuts land BEFORE/DURING the kill-box, so the squad
    is still alive at that point in the narrative.
    """
    STATE.mission2_squad = [
        {"name": "GABI",   "pos": list(REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS[0]), "alive": False, "activity": "down", "reaction": "down"},
        {"name": "SEBY",   "pos": list(REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS[1]), "alive": False, "activity": "down", "reaction": "down"},
        {"name": "DARIUS", "pos": list(REGROUP_TARGET_POSITIONS[2]), "alive": False, "activity": "down", "reaction": "down"},
    ]
    print(f"[SQUAD] Marked dead at kill-box positions: {[(a['name'], a['alive'], a['activity']) for a in STATE.mission2_squad]}")


def start_mission2_sniper(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2 and immediately jump to the sniper cutscene.

    Useful for testing the sniper sequence without the investigation phase.
    Usage: python acaristan.py --m2-sniper
    """
    player = start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)
    # Mark all clues as found so the sniper phase triggers on approach.
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        clue["done"] = True
        if clue.get("id"):
            STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
    # Also mark the truck manifest as inspected so the tanker stage is available.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_truck_manifest", None):
        STATE.mission2_truck_manifest["done"] = True
    # Set the state as if the flank cutscene has finished: sniper suppressed,
    # cleanup team ready to spawn, stage advanced to combat.
    _cleanup_sniper_state()
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "done"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_flank_cutscene_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_flank_cutscene_max = 0
    STATE.player_locked = False
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
    # Move the player near the flank route so they can immediately see the
    # cleanup team and truck without having to walk the whole gantry.
    if player:
        px, py = SNIPER_FLANK_POS
        player.rect.center = (px, py - 20)
        _snap_camera_to((px, py - 20))
    return player


def start_mission2_after_sniper(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2 and jump directly to the post-sniper cutscene combat.

    Skips the investigation phase AND the sniper cutscene. The player has
    Seby's OS-12 rifle, is in STAGE_COMBAT / get_cover phase, standing at
    the nearest killbox cover position. The initial IPA team (including
    the sniper) is spawned, so the full kill-box flow plays out from the
    moment you press a movement key.

    Usage: python acaristan.py --m2-after-sniper
    """
    player = start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        clue["done"] = True
        if clue.get("id"):
            STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
    grant_seby_os12()
    if player:
        px, py = KILLBOX_COVER_POSITIONS[0]
        player.rect.center = (px, py)
        _snap_camera_to((px, py))
    print("[SKIP] --m2-after-sniper: landed at STAGE_COMBAT / get_cover")
    return player


def start_mission2_flank(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2 and jump directly to the flank phase.

    Skips investigation, sniper cutscene, and the entire kill-box fight
    (get_cover → survive → clear). The player has the OS-12, the sniper
    is still alive on the gantry, and the post_sniper_phase is "flank".
    The player is placed right next to the south flank marker so the
    flank cutscene triggers within a second of walking.

    Usage: python acaristan.py --m2-flank
    """
    player = start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)
    _mark_squad_dead()  # post-kill-box: squad is dead at regroup positions
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        clue["done"] = True
        if clue.get("id"):
            STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
    grant_seby_os12()
    _spawn_initial_ipa_team()
    STATE.enemies[:] = [
        e for e in STATE.enemies if getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False)
    ]
    STATE.mission2_reinforcement_triggered = True
    STATE.mission2_reinforcement_waves = 3
    STATE.mission2_reinforcement_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_late_reveal_played = True
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "flank"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_flank_sniper"
    _set_objective("SNIPER STILL ACTIVE — flank to the orange circle")
    if player:
        fx, fy = KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS[0]
        player.rect.center = (fx, fy + 300)
        _snap_camera_to((fx, fy + 300))
    print("[SKIP] --m2-flank: landed at STAGE_COMBAT / flank phase")
    return player


def start_mission2_truck(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2 and jump directly to the truck inspection phase.

    Skips everything up to and including the flank cutscene + cleanup team
    fight. The player has the OS-12 with 2 spare mags (the cleanup-team
    reward), the sniper is suppressed/removed, and the stage is STAGE_TRUCK.
    The player is placed right next to the IPA truck so the manifest
    inspection triggers immediately.

    Usage: python acaristan.py --m2-truck
    """
    player = start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)
    _mark_squad_dead()  # post-kill-box: squad is dead at regroup positions
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        clue["done"] = True
        if clue.get("id"):
            STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
    grant_seby_os12()
    _spawn_initial_ipa_team()
    _cleanup_sniper_state()
    STATE.mission2_cleanup_team_spawned = True
    STATE.enemies[:] = []
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = ""
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_TRUCK
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_truck"
    _set_objective("Inspect the IPA truck manifest with [E]")
    # OS-12 spare mags are now granted at pickup (exactly 2) — no mid-mission
    # resupply. Scarcity is the design tension that makes the rifle feel earned.
    STATE.mission2_flank_cutscene_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
    STATE.player_locked = False
    if player:
        tx, ty = IPA_TRUCK_POS
        player.rect.center = (tx, ty + 80)
        _snap_camera_to((tx, ty + 80))
    print("[SKIP] --m2-truck: landed at STAGE_TRUCK (+2 OS 12 mags granted)")
    return player


def start_mission2_checkpoint(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2 at the evidence-phase checkpoint (post-tanker explosion).

    Called by restart_current_mission() when STATE.mission2_checkpoint_reached
    is True — i.e. the player survived the tanker blast, reached STAGE_EVIDENCE,
    and then died during the cleanup team fight. Instead of restarting the
    entire mission from the intro cutscene, this drops the player back at
    the shelter (SAFE_ZONE) with the evidence phase already active.

    State setup mirrors what the "blast_done" card close does in _close_card():
      - Stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
      - Player at SAFE_ZONE (the concrete shelter)
      - OS-12 granted with 2 spare mags
      - Sniper suppressed/removed
      - All clues marked as found
      - Cleanup team spawned (wave 1)
      - Persistent fire zones created (via _explode_tanker)
      - Player HP restored to 80 (survived the blast with debris scrape)
      - Checkpoint flag re-set (so subsequent deaths also respawn here)
    """
    player = start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)
    # ── CRITICAL: Re-set the checkpoint flag IMMEDIATELY after start_mission2() ──
    # start_mission2() calls STATE.reset(), which clears mission2_checkpoint_reached
    # to False. If we only re-set it at the end of this function (as the original
    # code did) and any intermediate call (grant_seby_os12, _explode_tanker,
    # _spawn_control_cleanup, etc.) throws an exception, the flag is left False.
    # The death-screen fallback handler then calls start_mission2() (NOT the
    # checkpoint variant), which also clears the flag — so the next death
    # restart does a full mission restart instead of a checkpoint respawn, or
    # worse, boots to main menu if the fallback also fails. Setting it here
    # ensures the flag survives any exception in the setup code below.
    STATE.mission2_checkpoint_reached = True
    # Suppress the investigation-phase intro radio line — wrong context for
    # the evidence phase checkpoint. We play a checkpoint-specific line instead.
    STATE._skip_mission2_intro_radio = True
    _mark_squad_dead()  # post-kill-box: squad is dead at regroup positions
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        clue["done"] = True
        if clue.get("id"):
            STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
    grant_seby_os12()
    _spawn_initial_ipa_team()
    _cleanup_sniper_state()
    STATE.enemies[:] = []
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = ""
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_evidence"
    _set_objective("Enter the control building and inspect green evidence terminals")
    # Checkpoint-specific Vasile line — replaces the wrong-context
    # "inspect the staged evidence" intro that start_mission2() would play.
    _radio('VASILE: "You made it. Control building — move. The evidence is inside."',
           color=C_VASILE, mark="checkpoint_respawn")
    # Re-create the persistent fire zones from the tanker explosion so the
    # evidence phase has the same environmental pressure as the first time.
    # _explode_tanker handles the fire zones + scorch + wreckage creation.
    # We call it here to recreate the post-blast state. The player is at the
    # shelter so they survive the "blast" damage check.
    if player:
        player.rect.center = SAFE_ZONE
        player.hp = 80  # post-blast debris scrape HP
    # Re-create the persistent fire zones from the tanker explosion so the
    # evidence phase has the same environmental pressure as the first time.
    # Wrapped in try/except: _explode_tanker() is the most complex call in
    # this function (audio scheduling, particle spawning, fire zone creation,
    # vehicle charring, debris field generation) and is the most likely throw
    # point. If it fails, the checkpoint respawn still works — the player just
    # won't have the persistent fire zones, which is an acceptable degradation
    # compared to losing the checkpoint entirely or booting to main menu.
    try:
        _explode_tanker()
    except Exception as _blast_err:
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        print(f"[CHECKPOINT] _explode_tanker() failed during checkpoint respawn: {_blast_err}")
        # Still mark the tanker as exploded so downstream code (draw routines,
        # stage updates) treats it as destroyed even if the blast setup was
        # only partially applied.
        STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded = True
        STATE.mission2_tanker_destroyed = True
    # _explode_tanker sets stage to STAGE_BLACKOUT; override back to EVIDENCE.
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_evidence"
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_input_freeze = 0
    # Restore HP AFTER _explode_tanker (which deals 20-40 debris damage even
    # at the shelter). The player should start the evidence phase at 80 HP.
    if player:
        player.hp = 80
    # Spawn the cleanup team (wave 1).
    STATE.mission2_events.discard("control_cleanup")  # allow re-spawn
    _spawn_control_cleanup()
    # Belt-and-suspenders re-set of the checkpoint flag. The canonical set
    # is at the top of this function (immediately after start_mission2()),
    # but we re-set here too in case any code path above unexpectedly cleared
    # it — this is the original location of the flag re-set.
    STATE.mission2_checkpoint_reached = True
    STATE.player_locked = False
    if player:
        player.rect.center = SAFE_ZONE
        _snap_camera_to(SAFE_ZONE)
    # ── Kill the _explode_tanker() aftermath so the respawn doesn't replay
    # the BLEVE symphony. _explode_tanker() (called above at line 1582)
    # sets a 130-frame white-out flash (gc_flash_timer), screenshake
    # (already cleared by _snap_camera_to), and ~12 scheduled audio events
    # plus a 480-loop rig_fire_burst. Without this cleanup, the player
    # respawns into a flashbang + 8+ minutes of fire roar.
    # Surgical variant (preserves the checkpoint radio line just queued
    # by _radio() at line 1572): stop only the fire roar loop and clear
    # the scheduled-events list.
    STATE.gc_flash_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_tanker_ripple_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_tanker_subflash_queue = []
    STATE.mission2_tanker_shake_decay = 0
    try:
        audio.stop("rig_fire_burst")
        audio.scheduled.clear()
    except Exception:
        pass
    print("[CHECKPOINT] Respawned at shelter — evidence phase active")
    return player


def start_mission2_evidence_debug(preserve_timer=False):
    """Start Mission 2 already past the tanker explosion, with all 3 IPA
    cleanup waves pre-cleared. Player can immediately walk around the
    control building picking up the 3 evidence files and upload them.

    Sets up the post-blast narrative state (tanker exploded, comms cut,
    OS-12 granted, all clues found) but DOES NOT call _explode_tanker —
    the burning wreckage + persistent fire zones would clutter the map
    for evidence testing. The tanker is marked "destroyed" so the
    draw routine doesn't render the intact tanker either.

    All 3 evidence waves are marked as spent (wave = 3, cleared = True)
    AND the "control_cleanup" event is added to STATE.mission2_events
    so _spawn_control_cleanup() early-outs even if something else
    triggers it.

    Usage: python acaristan.py --m2-evidence
    """
    player = start_mission2(preserve_timer=preserve_timer)
    # Suppress the investigation-phase intro radio line — wrong context.
    STATE._skip_mission2_intro_radio = True
    _mark_squad_dead()  # post-kill-box: squad is dead at regroup positions
    # Mark all investigation clues as found (investigation phase is over).
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        clue["done"] = True
        if clue.get("id"):
            STATE.mission2_clues_found.add(clue["id"])
    # Grant Seby's OS-12 (player survived the killbox + flank + cleanup).
    grant_seby_os12()
    # Initialize IPA team state, then immediately clear it — the player
    # is past all combat.
    _spawn_initial_ipa_team()
    _cleanup_sniper_state()
    STATE.enemies[:] = []
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = ""
    STATE.mission2_cleanup_team_spawned = True
    # Post-blast narrative state. We mark the tanker as destroyed + comms
    # cut so radio dialogue + draw routines know we're past the explosion,
    # but we DON'T call _explode_tanker — the fire zones + scorch would
    # clutter the map for evidence testing.
    STATE.mission2_tanker_exploded = True
    STATE.mission2_tanker_destroyed = True
    STATE.mission2_comms_cut = True
    STATE.mission2_tanker_inspected = True
    # Skip ALL 3 evidence waves — set wave counter to max + mark cleared.
    # _update_evidence_waves() early-outs when wave >= 3.
    STATE.mission2_evidence_wave = 3
    STATE.mission2_evidence_wave_cleared = True
    STATE.mission2_evidence_wave_timer = 0
    # Mark the cleanup event as fired so _spawn_control_cleanup won't run
    # even if something else triggers it.
    STATE.mission2_events.add("control_cleanup")
    # Activate the evidence stage.
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_EVIDENCE
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_evidence"
    _set_objective("Inspect green evidence terminals with [E] (0/3)")
    # Brief Vasile line — replaces the wrong-context intro.
    _radio('VASILE: "Cleanup team\'s down. Grab the evidence — three terminals, then upload."',
           color=C_VASILE, mark="debug_evidence_start")
    # Restore player HP — player survived prior combat at full strength.
    if player:
        player.hp = 100
        # Place the player just south of the first evidence marker
        # (DECREE at _tc(55, 23)) so they can immediately walk up and
        # press [E]. The control building entrance is south of the
        # evidence cluster, so this is also the natural approach path.
        first_ev = STATE.mission2_evidence[0] if STATE.mission2_evidence else None
        if first_ev:
            fx, fy = first_ev["pos"]
            player.rect.center = (fx, fy + 80)
            _snap_camera_to((fx, fy + 80))
        else:
            player.rect.center = CONTROL_ROOM
            _snap_camera_to(CONTROL_ROOM)
    # Set checkpoint so subsequent deaths respawn at the evidence phase
    # (via start_mission2_checkpoint). This is the canonical "you
    # reached the evidence phase" respawn point.
    STATE.mission2_checkpoint_reached = True
    STATE.player_locked = False
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_input_freeze = 0
    print("[SKIP] --m2-evidence: landed at STAGE_EVIDENCE (all 3 waves cleared)")
    return player


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Story scripting / interactions
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _clue_count():
    return sum(1 for c in STATE.mission2_clues if c.get("done"))


def _evidence_count():
    return sum(1 for e in STATE.mission2_evidence if e.get("done"))


def _activate_travel_trigger(trigger):
    trigger["triggered"] = True
    sound = trigger.get("sound")
    if sound:
        audio.play(sound, volume=trigger.get("volume", 0.2))
    dialogue = trigger.get("dialogue")
    if dialogue:
        _radio(dialogue, color=trigger.get("color", C_VASILE), duration=trigger.get("duration", 260), mark=trigger["id"])
    for prop in trigger.get("props", []):
        story_prop = dict(prop)
        story_prop.setdefault("life", -1)
        story_prop.setdefault("age", 0)
        story_prop.setdefault("source", trigger["id"])
        STATE.mission2_story_props.append(story_prop)
    _sync_story_prop_collisions()


def _update_travel_triggers():
    """One-shot environmental story beats between clue zones."""
    if (not STATE.player or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") != STAGE_INVESTIGATE
            or getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None) is not None):
        return
    player_pos = _player_pos()
    STATE.mission2_current_zone = get_zone_for_world_pos(player_pos)
    for trigger in getattr(STATE, "mission2_triggers", []):
        if trigger.get("triggered"):
            continue
        if trigger["rect"].collidepoint(player_pos):
            _activate_travel_trigger(trigger)


def _actor_collision_rect(x, y):
    return pygame.Rect(int(x - 12), int(y - 12), 24, 24)


def _actor_rect_collides(rect):
    for wall in _solid_walls():
        if rect.colliderect(wall):
            return True
    return False


def _try_actor_step(actor, dx, dy):
    if abs(dx) < 0.001 and abs(dy) < 0.001:
        return False
    moved = False
    x, y = actor["pos"]
    # X axis first.
    if abs(dx) > 0.001:
        nr = _actor_collision_rect(x + dx, y)
        if not _actor_rect_collides(nr):
            actor["pos"][0] = x + dx
            x = actor["pos"][0]
            moved = True
    # Y axis second.
    if abs(dy) > 0.001:
        nr = _actor_collision_rect(x, y + dy)
        if not _actor_rect_collides(nr):
            actor["pos"][1] = y + dy
            moved = True
    return moved


def _move_actor_towards(actor, target, rate=0.055, max_step=2.7):
    """Move scripted squad actors while respecting walls/vehicles/solid props.

    This is deliberately not full combat pathfinding.  It is a lightweight
    cinematic/ambient mover with axis collision and a small sidestep fallback so
    actors stop sliding through barriers but can still work around minor clutter.
    """
    if not actor.get("alive", True):
        return
    x, y = actor["pos"]
    dx = target[0] - x
    dy = target[1] - y
    dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)
    if dist < 1:
        return
    step = min(max_step, dist * rate)
    nx = dx / dist * step
    ny = dy / dist * step
    moved = _try_actor_step(actor, nx, ny)
    if not moved and dist > 8:
        # Try sliding around small props/barriers.  Alternating side preference
        # stops all three squadmates choosing the exact same detour.
        side = -1 if (hash(actor.get("name", "")) % 2) else 1
        px = -ny * side
        py = nx * side
        if not _try_actor_step(actor, px, py):
            _try_actor_step(actor, -px, -py)
    if not moved and dist > 8:
        # Broader escape — try multiple angles when stuck on adjacent barriers.
        for slide_angle in (0.5, -0.5, 1.0, -1.0, 1.5, -1.5):
            cs = math.cos(slide_angle)
            sn = math.sin(slide_angle)
            sx = nx * cs - ny * sn
            sy = nx * sn + ny * cs
            if _try_actor_step(actor, sx, sy):
                break


def _nearest_unfound_clue(max_dist=360):
    if not STATE.player:
        return None
    px, py = _player_pos()
    best = None
    best_d = max_dist
    for clue in getattr(STATE, "mission2_clues", []):
        if clue.get("done"):
            continue
        d = math.hypot(clue["pos"][0] - px, clue["pos"][1] - py)
        if d < best_d:
            best = clue
            best_d = d
    return best


def _update_squad_ambient():
    """Zone-post squad behavior: active, but not player-following.

    The team occupies authored posts in the same broad investigation zone instead
    of orbiting Biribel.  They still move, scan, and peel toward relevant clues,
    but they do not form a follow-the-player escort blob.
    """
    if not STATE.player or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") != STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        return

    px, py = _player_pos()
    zone = getattr(STATE, "mission2_current_zone", None) or get_zone_for_world_pos((px, py)) or "approach"
    tick = pygame.time.get_ticks()
    patrol_step = (tick // 3600) % 2

    zone_posts = {
        "approach": [
            [(_tc(10, 72), _tc(14, 70)), (_tc(16, 74), _tc(21, 72)), (_tc(11, 75), _tc(18, 76))],
            ["scanning road", "covering rear", "radio check"],
            [SNIPER_NEST_POS, _tc(8, 74), _tc(12, 70)],
        ],
        "blast_scene": [
            [(_tc(20, 59), _tc(25, 58)), (_tc(31, 61), _tc(38, 60)), (_tc(28, 55), _tc(33, 56))],
            ["checking markings", "covering road", "checking timer"],
            [_tc(20, 59), SNIPER_NEST_POS, _tc(33, 40)],
        ],
        "south_ditch": [
            [(_tc(12, 50), _tc(18, 49)), (_tc(20, 47), _tc(23, 53)), (_tc(13, 50), _tc(17, 52))],
            ["watching bank", "covering ditch", "tracking signal"],
            [_tc(20, 47), _tc(6, 49), _tc(13, 50)],
        ],
        "north_service": [
            [(_tc(51, 43), _tc(58, 42)), (_tc(55, 45), _tc(62, 47)), (_tc(61, 43), _tc(56, 48))],
            ["checking gate", "checking tread", "comms diag"],
            [SNIPER_NEST_POS, _tc(55, 45), _tc(61, 43)],
        ],
        "toll_plaza": [
            [(_tc(38, 33), _tc(35, 34)), (_tc(34, 37), _tc(40, 35)), (_tc(39, 36), _tc(37, 37))],
            ["watching gantry", "covering lane", "checking radio"],
            [SNIPER_NEST_POS, SNIPER_NEST_POS, FIELD_RADIO_POS],
        ],
        "east_control": [
            [(_tc(55, 23), _tc(58, 20)), (_tc(63, 22), _tc(64, 18)), (_tc(61, 16), _tc(59, 15))],
            ["covering door", "watching hall", "terminal check"],
            [_tc(51, 25), _tc(67, 9), UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS],
        ],
    }
    posts, activities, lookats = zone_posts.get(zone, zone_posts["approach"])

    # If Biribel is close to an unfound clue, the role-relevant teammate moves
    # to that clue, not to Biribel.
    clue = _nearest_unfound_clue(max_dist=380)
    chosen_posts = [post_pair[int(patrol_step)] for post_pair in posts]
    chosen_activities = list(activities)
    chosen_lookats = list(lookats)
    if clue:
        cx, cy = clue["pos"]
        kind = clue.get("kind")
        if kind in ("mag_paint", "range_card"):
            chosen_posts[0] = (cx - 38, cy + 26)
            chosen_activities[0] = "reading marks"
            chosen_lookats[0] = clue["pos"]
        elif kind in ("bootprints", "timer_fragment"):
            chosen_posts[1] = (cx + 38, cy + 22)
            chosen_activities[1] = "inspecting scene"
            chosen_lookats[1] = clue["pos"]
        elif kind == "radio":
            chosen_posts[2] = (cx + 34, cy + 18)
            chosen_activities[2] = "checking static"
            chosen_lookats[2] = clue["pos"]

    for actor, target, activity, look_at in zip(STATE.mission2_squad, chosen_posts, chosen_activities, chosen_lookats):
        actor["activity"] = activity
        actor["look_at"] = look_at
        _move_actor_towards(actor, target, rate=0.035, max_step=1.85)


def _sniper_phase_activity():
    """Per-frame activity pass for the cutscene squad.

    Only the second definition matters (the first was a leftover no-op stub
    from an earlier refactor). Any squadmate that has been killed during
    the cutscene is forced into the 'down' pose so the draw code picks the
    dead-sprite branch.
    """
    for a in STATE.mission2_squad:
        if not a.get("alive", True):
            a["activity"] = "down"


def _update_squad_actor_positions():
    squad = getattr(STATE, "mission2_squad", None)
    if not squad or len(squad) < 3:
        return

    gabi, seby, darius = squad[0], squad[1], squad[2]
    seby_activity = (seby.get("activity") or "").lower()
    darius_activity = (darius.get("activity") or "").lower()
    pt = int(getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0))

    # Seby has three phases: sprint (lunge to Gabi), drag (carry her to
    # cover), and post-drag. Capture sprint_origin ONLY when sprinting
    # begins, otherwise the drag interpolation starts from wherever Seby
    # happened to be when drag kicked in (not from the kneel pose).
    if "sprint" in seby_activity or "drag" in seby_activity or "kneel" in seby_activity:
        if "sprint" in seby_activity and "_sprint_origin" not in seby:
            seby["_sprint_origin"] = tuple(seby["pos"])
        if ("drag" in seby_activity or "kneel" in seby_activity) and "_corpse_origin" not in gabi:
            gabi["_corpse_origin"] = tuple(gabi["pos"])

        s_ox, s_oy = seby.get("_sprint_origin", seby["pos"])
        g_ox, g_oy = gabi.get("_corpse_origin", gabi["pos"])

        kneel_x, kneel_y = g_ox - 22, g_oy - 4
        cover_x, cover_y = g_ox - 70, g_oy + 18

        if "sprint" in seby_activity:
            start = _beat("movement_two", "seby_breaks", 80)
            end = _beat("movement_two", "seby_arrives", 180)
            t = max(0.0, min(1.0, (pt - start) / max(1, end - start)))
            t = 1.0 - (1.0 - t) ** 2
            seby["pos"] = [s_ox + (kneel_x - s_ox) * t,
                           s_oy + (kneel_y - s_oy) * t]

        elif "drag" in seby_activity or "kneel" in seby_activity:
            # Drag phase: lerp Seby from kneel pose to cover using the
            # phase timer, smoothstepped. The earlier code did a redundant
            # cover_lerp/drag_lerp read that was always None and got
            # overwritten on the next line.
            start = _beat("movement_two", "seby_arrives", 180)
            end = _beat("movement_two", "seby_drag_to_cover_end", 340)
            t = max(0.0, min(1.0, (pt - start) / max(1, end - start)))
            t = t * t * (3.0 - 2.0 * t)
            seby["pos"] = [kneel_x + (cover_x + 18 - kneel_x) * t,
                           kneel_y + (cover_y - 4 - kneel_y) * t]
            gabi["pos"] = [g_ox + (cover_x - g_ox) * t,
                           g_oy + (cover_y - g_oy) * t]

            if pt % 5 == 0:
                STATE.blood_decals.append([
                    gabi["pos"][0] + random.randint(-4, 4),
                    gabi["pos"][1] + random.randint(-2, 2),
                    90,
                    random.randint(8, 14),
                ])
    else:
        seby.pop("_sprint_origin", None)
        gabi.pop("_corpse_origin", None)

    if "reach" in darius_activity:
        if "_reach_origin" not in darius:
            darius["_reach_origin"] = tuple(darius["pos"])
        ox, oy = darius["_reach_origin"]
        darius["pos"] = [ox + 18, oy - 8]
    elif "_reach_origin" in darius and "down" not in darius_activity:
        ox, oy = darius["_reach_origin"]
        darius["pos"] = [ox, oy]


SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES = ["regroup", "movement_one", "movement_two", "movement_three"]


def _sniper_phase_index():
    name = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase", "regroup")
    return SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES.index(name) if name in SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES else 0

def _sniper_phase():
    return SNIPER_PHASES[_sniper_phase_index()]

def _sniper_phase_name():
    return getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase", "")

def _sniper_phase_event(name):
    events = getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_events_fired", None)
    if events is None:
        events = set()
        STATE.mission2_sniper_events_fired = events
    if name in events:
        return False
    events.add(name)
    return True


def _sniper_actor_pos(index):
    try:
        return tuple(STATE.mission2_squad[index]["pos"])
    except Exception:
        return KILL_ZONE


def _midpoint(a, b, t=0.5):
    return (
        a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * t,
        a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t,
    )


def _avg_points(points):
    if not points:
        return KILL_ZONE
    return (
        sum(p[0] for p in points) / len(points),
        sum(p[1] for p in points) / len(points),
    )


def _squad_focus(include_dead=True):
    pts = []
    for actor in getattr(STATE, "mission2_squad", []):
        if include_dead or actor.get("alive", True):
            pts.append(tuple(actor["pos"]))
    return _avg_points(pts) if pts else KILL_ZONE


def _sniper_camera_target(t, phase):
    """Anchor-based camera target — the camera holds fixed compositions."""
    if phase == "movement_one":
        if t < _beat("movement_one", "gabi_crack") - 25:
            return CAM_SQUAD_WIDE
        if t < _beat("movement_one", "gantry_hold"):
            return CAM_GABI_BODY          # hold completely still on the body
        if t < _beat("movement_one", "seby_react"):
            return CAM_GANTRY_REVEAL      # hold for ~200 frames on the gantry
        return CAM_SEBY_DRAG              # settle on Seby for the transition

    elif phase == "movement_two":
        if t < _beat("movement_two", "seby_crack") + 60:
            return CAM_SEBY_DRAG          # hold during the entire drag
        if t < _beat("movement_two", "darius_crack") + 40:
            return CAM_DARIUS_RADIO       # hold on Darius
        return CAM_DARIUS_RADIO

    elif phase == "movement_three":
        if STATE.player:
            return (STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery - 20)
        return KILL_ZONE

    return KILL_ZONE


def _advance_sniper_phase():
    idx = _sniper_phase_index() + 1
    if idx >= len(SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES):
        _finish_sniper_sequence()
        return
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase = SNIPER_PHASE_NAMES[idx]
    STATE.mission2_sniper_phase_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_events_fired = set()


def _start_sniper_aftermath():
    STATE.mission2_stage = STAGE_COMBAT
    STATE.mission_phase = "m2_killbox_get_cover"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_phase = "get_cover"
    STATE.mission2_post_sniper_timer = 0

    STATE.mission2_grace_timer = SNIPER_GRACE_FRAMES

    STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_target = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_suppressed = False

    STATE.mission2_sniper_lock = 0
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lock_max = SNIPER_LOCK_FRAMES
    STATE.mission2_sniper_warned = set()
    STATE.mission2_sniper_lethal_target = None
    STATE.mission2_sniper_kill_flash = 0
    STATE.mission2_cover_hold = 0

    STATE.mission2_input_freeze = 18
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None
    STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer = 0

    _spawn_initial_ipa_team()

    _set_objective("GET TO COVER — sniper has the lane")


def _finish_sniper_sequence():
    STATE.mission2_fade_out = 60
    STATE.mission2_weapon_pickup_active = False
    STATE.mission2_weapon_pickup_ready = False
    _start_sniper_aftermath()


def grant_seby_os12():
    """Give Biribel Seby's OS-12 rifle and switch to it.
    Idempotent — safe to call more than once."""
    if getattr(STATE, "_seby_os12_granted", False):
        return
    STATE._seby_os12_granted = True
    STATE.mission2_os12_acquired = True
    STATE.os12_unlocked = True
    STATE.current_weapon = "os12"
    audio.play("rifle_handle", volume=0.6)
    STATE.mission2_heartbeat_active = False
    STATE.mission2_tinnitus_timer = min(getattr(STATE, "mission2_tinnitus_timer", 0), 30)
    STATE.mission2_radio_muffle_timer = min(getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0), 30)
    _m3_start_killbox_combat()


def _m3_start_killbox_combat():
    """Kick off the Route 12 kill-box wave after Biribel takes the OS-12."""
    if getattr(STATE, "_m3_killbox_started", False):
        return
    STATE._m3_killbox_started = True
    _set_objective("HOLD THE ROAD — Clear the Krap patrol")
    _start_sniper_aftermath()
    try:
        audio.play("combat_sting", volume=0.4)
    except Exception:
        pass


def _nearest_killbox_cover():
    if not STATE.player:
        return KILLBOX_COVER_POSITIONS[0]
    px, py = _player_pos()
    return min(KILLBOX_COVER_POSITIONS, key=lambda p: math.hypot(p[0] - px, p[1] - py))


def _nearest_flank_marker():
    if not STATE.player:
        return KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS[0]
    px, py = _player_pos()
    return min(KILLBOX_FLANK_POSITIONS, key=lambda p: math.hypot(p[0] - px, p[1] - py))


def _sniper_alive():
    return any(getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False) and e.hp > 0 for e in STATE.enemies)


def _player_exposed_to_sniper():
    if (
        not STATE.player
        or not _sniper_alive()
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False)
        or getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "") != STAGE_COMBAT
    ):
        return False

    px, py = _player_pos()
    sx, sy = SNIPER_NEST_POS

    dist = math.hypot(px - sx, py - sy)

    # The sniper should not shoot across the entire giant map,
    # but if you are anywhere near the checkpoint/gantry road and visible,
    # you are in danger.
    if dist > SNIPER_MAX_LOS_RANGE:
        return False

    # If nothing blocks line-of-sight, the sniper sees you.
    # No tiny killbox rectangle. No magic safety just because you walked past it.
    return not _wall_blocks_line(sx, sy, px, py, _solid_walls())


def _player_in_meaningful_cover():
    """Player has been in cover long enough to count as safe.

    Earlier this reset cover_hold to 0 the instant the player peeked out,
    which made peek-and-pop tactics impossible — a single exposed frame
    wiped any accumulated safe-time. The decay version drains the counter
    gracefully when exposed (so a quick peek only costs a small penalty)
    and ramps it back up while in cover.
    """
    if not STATE.player:
        return False
    if _player_exposed_to_sniper():
        STATE.mission2_cover_hold = max(
            0, getattr(STATE, "mission2_cover_hold", 0) - SNIPER_LOCK_DECAY
        )
        return False
