        # deactivated after completion. We just show the prompt and start
        # the OS on [E].
        import upload_os
        if not upload_os.is_upload_os_active():
            if not _near(UPLOAD_TERMINAL_POS, 110):
                _set_objective("Return to the upload terminal")
            else:
                _set_objective("Press [E] at the upload terminal")
                if keys[pygame.K_e]:
                    upload_os.start_upload_os()
                    STATE.mission2_upload_started = True
    elif stage == STAGE_EXFIL:
        _set_objective("Reach the green exfil marker")
        if _near(EXFIL_POS, 92):
            _complete_mission2()


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Combat/update helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def _update_player(keys, solid_walls):
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    if STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_INVESTIGATE:
        STATE.player.speed = 3.45
    elif STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_RUN:
        STATE.player.speed = 4.45
    else:
        STATE.player.speed = 3.6

    mx, my = get_scaled_mouse()
    world_mx = mx + STATE.camera_x
    world_my = my + STATE.camera_y
    STATE.player.angle = math.atan2(world_my - STATE.player.rect.centery, world_mx - STATE.player.rect.centerx)

    dx = dy = 0
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_input_freeze", 0) > 0:
        keys_for_move = {pygame.K_w: False, pygame.K_s: False, pygame.K_a: False, pygame.K_d: False}
    else:
        keys_for_move = _last_keys
    if not _movement_locked():
        # Delta-time aware: speed is in px/frame, multiply by (60*dt).
        _dt_mul = 60.0 * STATE.dt
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_w]:
            dy -= STATE.player.speed * _dt_mul
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_s]:
            dy += STATE.player.speed * _dt_mul
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_a]:
            dx -= STATE.player.speed * _dt_mul
        if keys_for_move[pygame.K_d]:
            dx += STATE.player.speed * _dt_mul
    if dx and dy:
        dx *= 0.707
        dy *= 0.707
    STATE.player.move(dx, dy, solid_walls)

    if dx or dy:
        STATE._footstep_timer -= 1
        if STATE._footstep_timer <= 0:
            audio.play("footstep", volume=0.25)
            STATE._footstep_timer = 27 + random.randint(0, 8)


def _update_weapon_timers():
    if STATE.shot_cooldown > 0:
        STATE.shot_cooldown -= 1
    if STATE.is_reloading:
        STATE.reload_timer -= 1
        if STATE.reload_timer <= 0:
            STATE.is_reloading = False
            is_os12 = getattr(STATE, "current_weapon", "pistol") == "os12"
            if is_os12:
                if STATE.os12_spare_mags > 0:
                    STATE.os12_spare_mags -= 1
                    STATE.os12_ammo_in_mag = STATE.os12_mag_size
            else:
                if STATE.spare_mags > 0:
                    STATE.spare_mags -= 1
                    STATE.ammo_in_mag = STATE.mag_size

    # OS 12 separate cooldown
    if getattr(STATE, "os12_shot_cooldown", 0) > 0:
        STATE.os12_shot_cooldown -= 1

    # ── Weapon swap animation tick ──
    if getattr(STATE, "weapon_swap_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.weapon_swap_timer -= 1
        duration = getattr(STATE, "weapon_swap_duration", 24)
        midpoint = duration // 2
        # At midpoint: flip the actual weapon and play the handling sound
        if (STATE.weapon_swap_timer <= midpoint
                and not getattr(STATE, "weapon_swap_sound_played", False)
                and getattr(STATE, "weapon_swap_pending", None) is not None):
            STATE.current_weapon = STATE.weapon_swap_pending
            vol = 0.4 if STATE.current_weapon == "os12" else 0.3
            audio.play("rifle_handle", volume=vol)
            STATE.weapon_swap_sound_played = True
        # On completion: clear pending
        if STATE.weapon_swap_timer <= 0:
            STATE.weapon_swap_pending = None
            STATE.weapon_swap_sound_played = False

    # ── Full-auto: OS 12 fires while mouse button is held ──
    if (not STATE.player_dead and STATE.player
            and not STATE.is_reloading
            and getattr(STATE, "weapon_swap_timer", 0) <= 0
            and getattr(STATE, "current_weapon", "pistol") == "os12"
            and getattr(STATE, "os12_unlocked", False)):
        if pygame.mouse.get_pressed()[0]:
            if getattr(STATE, "os12_shot_cooldown", 0) <= 0:
                handle_shoot()
    if STATE.melee_active:
        STATE.melee_timer -= 1
        if STATE.player and not STATE.melee_hit_done and STATE.melee_timer <= 10:
            # Shared melee resolver: range, cone, LOS, damage, particles,
            # shake are all defined in player.resolve_melee. M2 previously
            # had no cone check and no LOS check — you could hit enemies
            # behind you and through walls. Now consistent with M1.
            hit_enemy = resolve_melee(
                STATE.player, STATE.enemies, _solid_walls(), C_BLOOD,
            )
            if hit_enemy is not None:
                STATE.melee_hit_done = True
                STATE.shots_hit += 1
        if STATE.melee_timer <= 0:
            STATE.melee_active = False
            STATE.melee_hit_done = False


def _update_molotovs(solid_walls):
    player_hit = False
    for b in STATE.thrown_bottles[:]:
        prev_x, prev_y = b["x"], b["y"]
        b["t"] = min(1.0, b["t"] + b["speed"])
        nx = b["start_x"] + (b["tx"] - b["start_x"]) * b["t"]
        ny = b["start_y"] + (b["ty"] - b["start_y"]) * b["t"]
        ix, iy = nx, ny
        hit_wall = False
        for wall in solid_walls:
            clipped = wall.clipline((prev_x, prev_y), (nx, ny))
            if clipped:
                ix, iy = clipped[0]
                hit_wall = True
                break
        if b["t"] >= 1.0 or hit_wall:
            _spawn_molotov_fire(ix, iy, solid_walls)
            STATE.thrown_bottles.remove(b)
        else:
            b["x"], b["y"] = nx, ny

    for fire in STATE.molotov_fires[:]:
        fire["life"] -= 1
        fx, fy = fire["x"], fire["y"]
        if random.random() < 0.55:
            STATE.particles.append(Particle(fx + random.randint(-55, 55), fy + random.randint(-45, 45), random.choice([(255, 95, 25), (255, 160, 45), (70, 65, 60)])))
        for e in STATE.enemies:
            d = math.hypot(e.rect.centerx - fx, e.rect.centery - fy)
            if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS and not _wall_blocks_line(fx, fy, e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, solid_walls):
                e.hp -= MOLOTOV_ENEMY_DAMAGE * (0.5 + (1 - d / MOLOTOV_RADIUS))
                e.alerted = True
                e.state = "CHASE"
        if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead and not _movement_locked():
            d = math.hypot(STATE.player.rect.centerx - fx, STATE.player.rect.centery - fy)
            if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS * 0.7 and not _wall_blocks_line(fx, fy, STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery, solid_walls):
                # AEGIS Pass E: route through take_damage() so i-frames apply.
                # Molotov fires tick damage every frame; without i-frames the
                # player could lose 100+ HP in a single step into a fire zone.
                if STATE.player.take_damage(MOLOTOV_PLAYER_DAMAGE):
                    player_hit = True
        if fire["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.molotov_fires.remove(fire)

    # ── Car-burning hook ──
    # If a molotov fire overlaps a civilian car (not the IPA truck, not
    # already burned), start it burning. The burn progresses while any
    # molotov fire overlaps the car; once burn_progress reaches 1.0 the
    # car flips to "burned" permanently and stops accepting new fire.
    # This lets the player torch cars at will for fun/sabotage.
    if STATE.molotov_fires:
        for v in getattr(STATE, "mission2_vehicles", []):
            # IPA truck + tanker-blast-charred cars + already-burned cars
            # are immune to molotov burning.
            if v.get("kind") == "ipa_truck":
                continue
            if v.get("blast_charred", False):
                continue
            if v.get("kind") == "burned" and not v.get("burning", False):
                continue
            # If already fully burned, skip.
            if v.get("burn_progress", 0.0) >= 1.0:
                continue
            vcx = v["rect"].centerx
            vcy = v["rect"].centery
            # Check if any active molotov fire overlaps the car.
            overlapping = False
            for fire in STATE.molotov_fires:
                d = math.hypot(fire["x"] - vcx, fire["y"] - vcy)
                if d < MOLOTOV_RADIUS + 20:
                    overlapping = True
                    break
            if overlapping:
                v["burning"] = True
                # Burn progresses over ~6 seconds of sustained fire overlap.
                v["burn_progress"] = min(1.0, v.get("burn_progress", 0.0) + 1.0 / 360.0)
                # Once fully burned, flip the kind to "burned" so the
                # drawing code renders the permanent wreck variant.
                if v["burn_progress"] >= 1.0:
                    v["kind"] = "burned"
                    v["burning"] = False
                    v["molotov_burned"] = True  # distinguish from blast-charred
            # If not overlapping but was burning, the burn pauses (doesn't
            # reverse) — the car stays at its current burn level. Real fire
            # would keep going once lit, but for gameplay this is fine and
            # avoids cars torching themselves without sustained fire.

    # ── Burn-progress effects: smoke + flame particles ──
    # While a car is actively burning (burning=True, not yet fully
    # burned), emit flame + smoke particles from it so the player sees
    # the car catch fire and burn.
    for v in getattr(STATE, "mission2_vehicles", []):
        if not v.get("burning", False):
            continue
        if v.get("burn_progress", 0.0) >= 1.0:
            continue
        bp = v["burn_progress"]
        vcx = v["rect"].centerx
        vcy = v["rect"].centery
        # Flame particles — more as the burn progresses. Throttled.
        flame_rate = 1 + int(bp * 3)
        if STATE.mission2_timer % 2 == 0:
            for _ in range(flame_rate):
                col = random.choice([
                    (255, 180, 60), (255, 140, 40),
                    (255, 100, 30), (255, 220, 100),
                ])
                p = Particle(vcx + random.randint(-30, 30),
                             vcy + random.randint(-12, 12), col)
                p.dx = random.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)
                p.dy = random.uniform(-2.8, -1.2)
                p.life = random.randint(30, 70)
                STATE.particles.append(p)
        # Smoke particles — thicker as the burn progresses.
        if STATE.mission2_timer % 4 == 0:
            for _ in range(1 + int(bp * 2)):
                col = random.choice([(28, 24, 22), (45, 40, 35), (60, 52, 45)])
                p = Particle(vcx + random.randint(-35, 35),
                             vcy + random.randint(-16, 8), col)
                p.dx = random.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
                p.dy = random.uniform(-3.0, -1.5)
                p.life = random.randint(120, 220)
                STATE.particles.append(p)

    if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
        STATE.fire_zones[:] = [fz for fz in STATE.fire_zones if fz.update(STATE.enemies, STATE.player.rect, audio)]
    return player_hit


def _update_sniper_enemy(e, solid_walls):
    """Mission 2 sniper: fixed nest, long sightline, deliberate shots."""
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    px, py = _player_pos()
    dx = px - e.rect.centerx
    dy = py - e.rect.centery
    e.angle = math.atan2(dy, dx)
    if e.fire_cooldown > 0:
        e.fire_cooldown -= 1
    if _wall_blocks_line(e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, px, py, solid_walls):
        return
    if e.fire_cooldown <= 0:
        dist = max(1.0, math.hypot(dx, dy))
        vx, vy = dx / dist, dy / dist
        STATE.bullets.append({
            "x": e.rect.centerx + vx * 22,
            "y": e.rect.centery + vy * 22,
            "prev_x": e.rect.centerx + vx * 22,
            "prev_y": e.rect.centery + vy * 22,
            "dx": vx * 28,
            "dy": vy * 28,
            "life": 34,
            "owner": "enemy",
            "damage": 26,
        })
        STATE.mission2_sniper_traces.append({"from": (e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery), "to": (px, py), "life": 10})
        audio.play("gunshot_distant", volume=0.55)
        e.fire_cooldown = 115 + random.randint(0, 45)


def _update_bullets_enemies(solid_walls):
    player_hit = False
    for b in STATE.bullets[:]:
        b["prev_x"], b["prev_y"] = b["x"], b["y"]
        # Delta-time aware: dx/dy are in px/frame, multiply by (60*dt).
        _dt_mul = 60.0 * STATE.dt
        b["x"] += b["dx"] * _dt_mul
        b["y"] += b["dy"] * _dt_mul
        b["life"] -= 1
        # ── Entity hits checked BEFORE wall hits ──
        # Previously wall collision ran first, which meant a bullet that
        # reached the player's hitbox (22px radius) but was also inside a
        # cover rect (common when the player spawns behind sandbags or
        # leans against a wall) was absorbed as a "wall hit" and the
        # player took no damage. Now entity hits take priority — if a
        # bullet reaches an entity, it hits that entity, not the cover.
        hit = False
        # ── Player bullets vs enemies ──
        if not hit and b.get("owner") == "player":
            for e in STATE.enemies:
                if math.hypot(b["x"] - e.rect.centerx, b["y"] - e.rect.centery) < 22:

                    # You cannot pistol-snipe the sniper from the open road.
                    # If your bullet can reach him, he has already seen you.
                    if getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False) and not getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_suppressed", False):
                        hit = True

                        STATE.mission2_sniper_pressure_timer = 0
                        STATE.mission2_sniper_pending_timer = min(
                            getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_pending_timer", 999),
                            SNIPER_CLOSE_AIM_FRAMES,
                        )
                        STATE.mission2_sniper_target = _player_pos()
                        _add_sniper_laser(STATE.mission2_sniper_target, life=20)

                        STATE.floating_texts.append([
                            STATE.player.rect.centerx,
                            STATE.player.rect.centery - 52,
                            "SNIPER HAS YOUR ANGLE",
                            (255, 60, 40),
                            70,
                        ])

                        _burst(b["x"], b["y"], (140, 135, 120), 10, speed_mul=1.4)
                        audio.play("concrete_impact", volume=0.45)
                        break

                    e.hp -= b.get("damage", 50)
                    e.on_hit(STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery)
                    hit = True
                    STATE.shots_hit += 1
                    STATE.blood_decals.append([b["x"], b["y"], 140, random.randint(8, 16)])
                    _burst(b["x"], b["y"], C_BLOOD, 14)
                    # Spatial audio for enemy-hit (was flat audio.play(...,0.42)).
                    effects.play_impact(
                        "bullet_impact_enemy", (b["x"], b["y"]), base_volume=0.42,
                    )
                    break
        if not hit and b.get("owner") == "enemy" and STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
            if math.hypot(b["x"] - STATE.player.rect.centerx, b["y"] - STATE.player.rect.centery) < 22:
                # AEGIS Pass E: route through take_damage() so i-frames apply.
                # The mission2_grace_timer (post-sniper narrative protection)
                # is preserved as the outer gate — if a grace window is
                # active, neither damage nor i-frame logic runs. Inside the
                # grace-expired path, take_damage() adds tactical i-frames
                # on top, so the player can't be chain-melted by overlapping
                # enemy fire. If i-frames absorb the hit, player_hit stays
                # False (no damage feedback for an absorbed hit).
                if getattr(STATE, "mission2_grace_timer", 0) <= 0:
                    if STATE.player.take_damage(b.get("damage", ENEMY_DAMAGE)):
                        player_hit = True
                hit = True
                # Shared max-clamp shake trigger (was max(...) inline; now via
                # effects.shake for consistency with the rest of the campaign).
                effects.shake(6)
                _burst(b["x"], b["y"], C_BLOOD, 8)
                audio.play("bullet_impact_enemy", volume=0.5)
        # ── Wall hits checked LAST (only if no entity was hit) ──
        # This is the fix for "player not taking damage": previously the wall
        # check ran first and absorbed any bullet that was inside a cover rect,
        # even if the bullet had reached the player's hitbox. Now entity hits
        # take priority; wall hits only happen for bullets that missed everyone.
        if not hit:
            hit = collide_bullet_walls(b, solid_walls)
            if hit:
                _burst(b["x"], b["y"], (130, 125, 110), 4)
                effects.play_impact("bullet_impact", (b["x"], b["y"]), base_volume=0.30)
        if hit or b["life"] <= 0 or not (-500 < b["x"] < MAP_W * TILE_SIZE + 500 and -500 < b["y"] < MAP_H * TILE_SIZE + 500):
            if b in STATE.bullets:
                STATE.bullets.remove(b)

    for e in STATE.enemies[:]:
        if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
            e.alerted = True
            if getattr(e, "is_mission2_sniper", False):
                # Mission 2's post-ambush sniper is handled by the authored
                # kill-box pressure system, not generic enemy fire. Keep the
                # actor facing the player for drawing/feedback.
                px, py = _player_pos()
                e.angle = math.atan2(py - e.rect.centery, px - e.rect.centerx)
            else:
                if e.state in ("IDLE", "PATROL", "SEARCH"):
                    e.state = "CHASE"
                e.last_seen_pos = _player_pos()
                e.update(STATE.player.rect.centerx, STATE.player.rect.centery, solid_walls, STATE.bullets, STATE.particles, STATE.enemies, STATE.fire_zones)
        if e.hp <= 0:
            STATE.hostiles_killed += 1
            STATE.blood_decals.append([e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, 165, random.randint(12, 22)])
            _burst(e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, C_BLOOD, 24)
            roll = random.random()
            if roll < 0.28:
                STATE.pickups.append([e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, "bandage"])
            elif roll < 0.56 and STATE.spare_mags < 5:
                STATE.pickups.append([e.rect.centerx, e.rect.centery, "mag"])
            STATE.enemies.remove(e)
    return player_hit


def _update_pickups():
    if not STATE.player or STATE.player_dead:
        return
    for pu in STATE.pickups[:]:
        if math.hypot(pu[0] - STATE.player.rect.centerx, pu[1] - STATE.player.rect.centery) > 32:
            continue
        kind = pu[2]
        if kind == "bandage":
            old = STATE.player.hp
            STATE.player.hp = min(STATE.player.max_hp, STATE.player.hp + 35)
            STATE.floating_texts.append([pu[0], pu[1] - 16, f"+{int(STATE.player.hp - old)} HP", (100, 220, 100), 70])
        elif kind == "mag":
            STATE.spare_mags = min(5, STATE.spare_mags + 1)
            STATE.floating_texts.append([pu[0], pu[1] - 16, "+1 MAG", C_TERMINAL, 70])
        audio.play("pickup", volume=0.45)
        STATE.pickups.remove(pu)


def _update_particles_texts(solid_walls):
    for p in STATE.particles[:]:
        p.update()
        if p.life <= 0:
            STATE.particles.remove(p)
    for c in STATE.casings[:]:
        c.update(solid_walls)
        if c.life <= 0:
            STATE.casings.remove(c)
    for ft in STATE.floating_texts[:]:
        if len(ft) < 6 or ft[5] != "pin":
            ft[1] -= 1.0
        ft[4] -= 1
        if ft[4] <= 0:
            STATE.floating_texts.remove(ft)
    for tr in STATE.mission2_sniper_traces[:]:
        tr["life"] -= 1
        if tr["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_traces.remove(tr)
    for laser in getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_lasers", [])[:]:
        laser["life"] -= 1
        if laser["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_lasers.remove(laser)
    for flash in getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes", [])[:]:
        flash["life"] -= 1
        if flash["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_sniper_muzzle_flashes.remove(flash)
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_tinnitus_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_tinnitus_timer -= 1

    # --- Tanker explosion ongoing effects ---
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # POST-BLAST AFTERMATH — runs for the rest of the mission whenever
    # the tanker has exploded, regardless of stage. Previously this whole
    # block was gated on STAGE_BLACKOUT, which meant the moment the player
    # closed the "COMMS LOST" card and transitioned to STAGE_EVIDENCE,
    # ALL ambient smoke/embers stopped instantly AND any in-flight debris
    # chunks froze in mid-air. That's why the aftermath felt underwhelming
    # — the blast site went from "roaring inferno" to "slightly scorched
    # ground with campfires" in a single frame.
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_exploded", False):
        STATE.mission2_tanker_blast_timer = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_blast_timer", 0) + 1
        bt = STATE.mission2_tanker_blast_timer

        # --- Heavy debris chunk physics. Runs every frame so in-flight
        # chunks keep moving and landed chunks keep bouncing until their
        # life expires, even after the stage transitions away from
        # STAGE_BLACKOUT. ---
        new_chunks = []
        for chunk in getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_debris_chunks", []):
            chunk["x"] += chunk["dx"]
            chunk["y"] += chunk["dy"]
            chunk["dy"] += 0.25   # slightly heavier gravity for bigger chunks
            chunk["dx"] *= 0.995
            chunk["age"] += 1
            chunk["life"] -= 1
            if chunk["y"] > chunk["ground_y"] and chunk["dy"] > 0:
                chunk["y"] = chunk["ground_y"]
                chunk["dy"] *= -0.3
                chunk["dx"] *= 0.55
                # Bigger dust puff for bigger chunks.
                if abs(chunk["dy"]) > 1.0:
                    for _ in range(6):
                        p = Particle(int(chunk["x"]) + random.randint(-8, 8),
                                     int(chunk["y"]) + random.randint(-3, 3),
                                     (90, 80, 70))
                        p.dx = random.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)
                        p.dy = random.uniform(-2.2, -0.3)
                        p.life = random.randint(20, 40)
                        STATE.particles.append(p)
            if chunk["life"] > 0:
                new_chunks.append(chunk)
        STATE.mission2_tanker_debris_chunks = new_chunks

        # --- Persistent aftermath smoke + embers. Runs FOREVER (for the
        # rest of the mission) so the blast site keeps visibly smoldering
        # during STAGE_EVIDENCE / STAGE_UPLOAD / STAGE_EXFIL. During
        # STAGE_BLACKOUT the heavier emitters below dominate; this block
        # carries the aftermath once those fade out. Rate is throttled
        # (every 6th frame for smoke, every 8th frame for embers) so it
        # stays cheap but never goes silent. ---
        if not STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_BLACKOUT or bt >= 600:
            tanker = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
            cx_sm, cy_sm = tanker.centerx, tanker.centery
            # Smoke — 2-3 particles every 6 frames, drifting up and out.
            if bt % 6 == 0:
                for _ in range(3):
                    col = random.choice([(25, 22, 20), (40, 35, 32), (55, 48, 42)])
                    p = Particle(cx_sm + random.randint(-45, 45),
                                 cy_sm + random.randint(-25, 5), col)
                    p.dx = random.uniform(-0.8, 0.8)
                    p.dy = random.uniform(-2.8, -1.2)
                    p.life = random.randint(200, 320)
                    STATE.particles.append(p)
            # Embers — 1-2 particles every 8 frames, glowing orange/red.
            if bt % 8 == 0:
                for _ in range(2):
                    col = random.choice([
                        (255, 180, 60), (255, 140, 40), (255, 100, 30), (255, 80, 25),
                    ])
                    p = Particle(cx_sm + random.randint(-90, 90),
                                 cy_sm + random.randint(-30, 50), col)
                    p.dx = random.uniform(-0.5, 0.5)
                    p.dy = random.uniform(-3.2, -1.2)
                    p.life = random.randint(50, 110)
                    STATE.particles.append(p)

        # --- Outer spot fires — emit fire + smoke particles from each
        # persistent spot fire (embers that landed 250-600px out and
        # ignited secondary blazes). Throttled so 12 fires stay cheap.
        # Runs forever so the wider scene keeps burning. ---
        spot_fires = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_spot_fires", [])
        if spot_fires and bt % 5 == 0:
            for sf in spot_fires:
                # Fire particles — orange/red, rising.
                col = random.choice([
                    (255, 160, 50), (255, 120, 35), (255, 90, 25), (255, 200, 80),
                ])
                p = Particle(sf["x"] + random.randint(-6, 6),
                             sf["y"] + random.randint(-4, 4), col)
                p.dx = random.uniform(-0.4, 0.4)
                p.dy = random.uniform(-2.2, -1.0)
                p.life = random.randint(25, 55)
                STATE.particles.append(p)
                # Occasional smoke wisp from each spot fire.
                if bt % 15 == 0:
                    sp = Particle(sf["x"] + random.randint(-8, 8),
                                  sf["y"] + random.randint(-6, 2),
                                  random.choice([(30, 27, 24), (45, 40, 35)]))
                    sp.dx = random.uniform(-0.5, 0.5)
                    sp.dy = random.uniform(-1.8, -0.8)
                    sp.life = random.randint(90, 160)
                    STATE.particles.append(sp)

    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    # ACTIVE BLAST — heavy one-shot effects that only make sense during
    # the cinematic STAGE_BLACKOUT window: screen shake, shockwave ring
    # growth, fireball growth, BLEVE subflashes, the initial 600-frame
    # heavy smoke plume, and the big ember rise. Once the player closes
    # the "COMMS LOST" card and the stage transitions to EVIDENCE, this
    # block stops running and only the persistent aftermath above remains.
    # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_exploded", False) and STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_BLACKOUT:
        bt = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_blast_timer", 0)

        # Screen shake decay — 160 peak over 240 frames.
        if getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_shake_decay", 0) > 0:
            decay_remaining = STATE.mission2_tanker_shake_decay
            shake_val = int(160 * (decay_remaining / 240.0))
            STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, shake_val)
            STATE.mission2_tanker_shake_decay -= 1

        # Screen ripple timer — drives the horizontal wobble distortion
        # drawn in _draw_blackout_overlay. Decays over 40 frames.
        if getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_ripple_timer", 0) > 0:
            STATE.mission2_tanker_ripple_timer -= 1

        # Shockwave ring: 1600px max radius over 50 frames (was 1100/40).
        # This actually fills the screen and beyond.
        if bt <= 50:
            STATE.mission2_tanker_shockwave_radius = int(bt * 32.0)  # 1600px max

        # Fireball core radius: 700px max over 45 frames (was 280/35).
        # This is the screen-dominating white-hot sphere.
        if bt <= 45:
            STATE.mission2_tanker_fireball_radius = int(bt * 15.5)  # ~700px max

        # Secondary debris wave + heavy chunks (90 chunks, was 34).
        pending_debris = getattr(STATE, "mission2_pending_debris_wave", 0)
        if pending_debris > 0:
            STATE.mission2_pending_debris_wave = pending_debris - 1
            if pending_debris == 1:
                tanker = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
                cx, cy = tanker.centerx, tanker.centery
                # Lightweight spray (350 particles, matches _spawn_debris).
                for _ in range(350):
                    col = random.choice([
                        (60, 55, 50), (40, 35, 30), (95, 88, 76),
                        (140, 40, 32), (75, 30, 25),
                    ])
                    p = Particle(cx + random.randint(-70, 70), cy + random.randint(-40, 40), col)
                    angle = random.uniform(0, math.tau)
                    speed = random.uniform(2.0, 5.5)
                    p.dx = math.cos(angle) * speed
                    p.dy = math.sin(angle) * speed
                    p.life = random.randint(60, 160)
                    STATE.particles.append(p)
                # Heavy debris chunks — 90 of them, with bigger sizes.
                for _ in range(90):
                    angle = random.uniform(-math.pi, 0.0)  # bias upward
                    speed = random.uniform(5.0, 11.0)
                    chunk = {
                        "x": cx + random.randint(-40, 40),
                        "y": cy + random.randint(-30, 10),
                        "dx": math.cos(angle) * speed,
                        "dy": math.sin(angle) * speed,
                        "size": random.randint(5, 12),  # bigger chunks
                        "color": random.choice([
                            (60, 55, 50), (40, 35, 30), (95, 88, 76),
                            (140, 40, 32), (75, 30, 25),
                        ]),
                        "age": 0,
                        "life": random.randint(100, 200),
                        "ground_y": cy + random.randint(20, 60),
                    }
                    STATE.mission2_tanker_debris_chunks.append(chunk)

        # Subflash triggers — 5 BLEVE secondaries at t=15, 30, 50, 75, 105.
        subflash_q = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_subflash_queue", [])
        if subflash_q and bt >= subflash_q[0]:
            subflash_q.pop(0)
            STATE.gc_flash_timer = max(getattr(STATE, "gc_flash_timer", 0), 30)
            STATE.screen_shake = max(getattr(STATE, "screen_shake", 0), 75)
            STATE.cam_kick_x += random.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * 20.0
            STATE.cam_kick_y += random.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * 20.0
            STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_chromatic_timer", 0), 80)
            STATE.mission2_tanker_ripple_timer = max(getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_ripple_timer", 0), 12)
            # Fresh ember burst — bigger on each secondary.
            tanker = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
            cx2, cy2 = tanker.centerx, tanker.centery
            for _ in range(100):
                col = random.choice([
                    (255, 255, 230), (255, 220, 140), (255, 160, 50), (255, 95, 20),
                ])
                p = Particle(cx2 + random.randint(-60, 60), cy2 + random.randint(-40, 20), col)
                angle = random.uniform(0, math.tau)
                speed = random.uniform(3.0, 7.5)
                p.dx = math.cos(angle) * speed
                p.dy = math.sin(angle) * speed
                p.life = random.randint(30, 80)
                STATE.particles.append(p)

        # Continuous heavy smoke plume — emit for 600 frames (10s) during
        # the blackout. After bt >= 600 OR after the stage transitions,
        # the persistent-aftermath block above takes over at a lower rate.
        if bt < 600 and bt % 2 == 0:
            tanker = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
            cx, cy = tanker.centerx, tanker.centery
            for _ in range(7):
                col = random.choice([(15, 13, 12), (22, 20, 18), (35, 30, 28), (55, 48, 42)])
                p = Particle(cx + random.randint(-60, 60), cy + random.randint(-40, 10), col)
                p.dx = random.uniform(-1.2, 1.2)
                p.dy = random.uniform(-3.5, -1.5)
                p.life = random.randint(180, 320)
                STATE.particles.append(p)

        # Big ember rise during the active burn — denser and wider than
        # the persistent aftermath emitters.
        if bt > 20 and bt % 2 == 0:
            tanker = STATE.mission2_tanker_rect
            cx, cy = tanker.centerx, tanker.centery
            for _ in range(5):
                col = random.choice([(255, 200, 80), (255, 180, 60), (255, 120, 40), (255, 90, 30)])
                p = Particle(cx + random.randint(-150, 150), cy + random.randint(-40, 70), col)
                p.dx = random.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)
                p.dy = random.uniform(-4.0, -1.5)
                p.life = random.randint(60, 120)
                STATE.particles.append(p)
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_kill_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_kill_flash -= 1
    props_changed = False
    for prop in getattr(STATE, "mission2_story_props", [])[:]:
        prop["age"] = prop.get("age", 0) + 1
        if prop.get("life", -1) > 0:
            prop["life"] -= 1
            if prop["life"] <= 0:
                STATE.mission2_story_props.remove(prop)
                props_changed = True
    if props_changed:
        _sync_story_prop_collisions()
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_flash -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_near_miss_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_near_miss_flash -= 1

    callout = getattr(STATE, "mission2_death_callout", None)
    if callout:
        callout["life"] -= 1
        if callout["life"] <= 0:
            STATE.mission2_death_callout = None


def _update_camera():
    # M2-specific target selection: cutscene focus overrides player; cutscene
    # snap overrides both. Then delegate the actual shake / kick / lerp /
    # clamp / jitter to the shared camera.update_camera so M1 and M2 can't
    # drift on those axes again.
    target = STATE.mission2_camera_focus or _player_pos()
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_snap", None):
        target = STATE.mission2_camera_snap
        STATE.mission2_camera_snap = None

    # M2 used to keep its own first-frame-snap flag (mission2_camera_initialized).
    # The shared camera uses STATE.camera_initialized instead. Migrate the flag
    # so the snap still fires on the first frame after start_mission2.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_initialized", False):
        STATE.camera_initialized = True
        STATE.mission2_camera_initialized = False  # one-shot migration

    # Per-stage lerp strategy:
    #   - Sniper stage: distance-scaled smoothstep (slow start, slow end, fast middle).
    #     Preserved from the original M2 implementation — this is the
    #     carefully-tuned "tense camera" feel for the Route 12 ambush.
    #   - Cutscene focus pull: constant 0.38 ease (snappy).
    #   - Default gameplay: constant 0.14 ease (slightly slower than M1's 0.15
    #     to preserve M2's deliberately weightier feel).
    stage = getattr(STATE, "mission2_stage", "")
    in_sniper = stage == STAGE_SNIPER
    focus_active = getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_focus_timer", 0) > 0

    if in_sniper:
        def _smoothstep_factor(dx, dy, dist):
            f = min(1.0, 0.08 * (1.0 + dist / 600.0))
            return f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f)
        factor_fn = _smoothstep_factor
        focus_factor = None
    elif focus_active:
        factor_fn = None
        focus_factor = 0.38
    else:
        factor_fn = None
        focus_factor = 0.14

    handheld = (getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_phase_timer", 0)
                if in_sniper else None)

    return update_camera(
        target,
        map_w=MAP_W, map_h=MAP_H,
        focus_factor=focus_factor,
        factor_fn=factor_fn,
        handheld_jitter_timer=handheld,
    )


def game_update(keys):
    global _last_keys
    _last_keys = keys
    # AEGIS Pass E: advance player i-frame timer + sprite blink every frame.
    # Must run BEFORE the death-trigger check below so the timer ticks down
    # even on the frame the player dies (otherwise a freshly-set i-frame
    # window would freeze the blink state on the death frame).
    if STATE.player:
        STATE.player.update_iframes(STATE.dt)
    if STATE.gc_flash_timer > 0:
        STATE.gc_flash_timer -= 1
    if STATE.player and STATE.player.hp <= 0 and not STATE.player_dead:
        # Death animation (Option B — Standard) handles the full sequence:
        # slow-mo, camera collapse, ragdoll, blood pool, tinnitus, radio cut.
        # Replaces the legacy single-burst + shake trigger.
        import death_anim
        death_anim.trigger_death()
    if STATE.player_dead:
        import death_anim
        ticks = death_anim.update_death_anim()
        STATE._death_logic_ticks = ticks
    else:
        STATE._death_logic_ticks = 1

    # ── Grace timer countdown ──
    # This was previously inside _update_sniper_pressure(), which only runs
    # during STAGE_COMBAT with the sniper alive. Once the player advanced
    # past the sniper (STAGE_EVIDENCE, STAGE_TRUCK, etc.), the grace timer
    # never counted down — it stayed at 90 forever, blocking ALL enemy bullet
    # damage during the cleanup team fight. Moving it here ensures it ticks
    # down regardless of stage. The timer is only SET during the sniper phase.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_grace_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_grace_timer -= 1

    # ── Evidence completion cinematic (Option E) ──
    # When active, this takes priority over normal gameplay updates —
    # the player is watching a cinematic, not fighting. We still update
    # particles + camera so the scene looks alive, but we skip enemy AI,
    # bullets, etc.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_evidence_cinematic_active", False):
        _update_evidence_cinematic()
        # Update particles + floating texts so the scene doesn't freeze.
        solid_walls = _solid_walls()
        effects.update_particles()
        effects.update_floating_texts()
        return STATE.camera_x, STATE.camera_y

    # Post-cinematic grace period — enemies stay frozen for 0.5s after the
    # cinematic ends so the player can orient before combat resumes.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_evidence_cinematic_grace", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_evidence_cinematic_grace -= 1
        # Still update player movement + camera, but skip enemy AI + bullets.
        solid_walls = _solid_walls()
        _update_player(keys, solid_walls)
        effects.update_particles()
        effects.update_floating_texts()
        return STATE.camera_x, STATE.camera_y

    # ── Upload OS (Option: Acarizwork upload) ──
    # When the upload OS is active, freeze enemies + skip gameplay updates.
    # The OS handles its own input via handle_upload_os_event (called from
    # acaristan.py's event loop). Here we update the OS frame state (boot
    # reveal, upload progress, done timer) and check for completion.
    #
    # CRITICAL: This MUST be done here, NOT in _update_stage(). When the OS
    # is active, this branch returns early — _update_stage() is never called.
    # Previously the update_upload_os() call lived in _update_stage()'s
    # STAGE_UPLOAD branch, which was unreachable when the OS was active.
    # That left _os_timer stuck at 0: boot lines never revealed, upload
    # progress never filled, done timer never advanced. The player saw a
    # frozen black screen with scanlines.
    import upload_os
    if upload_os.is_upload_os_active():
        mouse_pos = pygame.mouse.get_pos()
        upload_os.update_upload_os(keys, mouse_pos)
        # ── Vasile radio warning at ~50% upload progress ──
        # Fires once, when the upload crosses 50%. Uses _vasile_warning_played
        # flag (reset in start_upload_os) to ensure it only plays once.
        if (not upload_os._vasile_warning_played
                and upload_os._upload_progress >= 0.5
                and upload_os._os_phase == upload_os.PHASE_UPLOAD
                and not upload_os._upload_intrusion_active):
            upload_os._vasile_warning_played = True
            _radio('VASILE: "Hurry. They\'re triangulating."',
                   color=(255, 150, 80), mark="upload_warning",
                   allow_when_cut=True)
            audio.play("radio_static", volume=0.3)
        # Check if the upload completed — if so, deactivate the OS and
        # open the "TRANSMISSION SENT" card. The card's `after="upload_done"`
        # handler transitions to STAGE_EXFIL when closed.
        if upload_os.is_upload_complete():
            upload_os.deactivate_upload_os()  # plays CRT thunk + restores cursor
            _open_card(
                "TRANSMISSION SENT",
                [
                    "The Route 12 evidence package is out.",
                    "IPA corrupted part of the archive, but enough survived.",
                    "Move to extraction before the state broadcast lands.",
                ],
                color=C_TERMINAL,
                after="upload_done",
                kind="terminal",
            )
        effects.update_particles()
        effects.update_floating_texts()
        return STATE.camera_x, STATE.camera_y

    solid_walls = _solid_walls()
    _update_player(keys, solid_walls)
    _update_travel_triggers()
    _update_squad_ambient()
    _update_weapon_timers()
    player_hit = _update_molotovs(solid_walls)
    if STATE.mission2_stage not in (STAGE_SNIPER, STAGE_BLACKOUT, STAGE_DONE) and not getattr(STATE, "mission2_card", None):
        player_hit = _update_bullets_enemies(solid_walls) or player_hit
    if player_hit:
        STATE.screen_shake = max(STATE.screen_shake, 4)
    _update_pickups()
    _update_stage(keys)
    _update_particles_texts(solid_walls)

    _update_flank_cutscene()
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_chromatic_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_chromatic_timer -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_sniper_silhouette_flash -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_radio_muffle_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_radio_muffle_timer -= 1
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_input_freeze", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_input_freeze -= 1
    # Camera focus timer ticks down here so the heavy ease (0.38) returns
    # to default (0.14) once the focus event has passed. Previously the
    # timer was only set, never decremented, leaving the camera in
    # high-ease mode for the entire post-cutscene gameplay.
    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_camera_focus_timer", 0) > 0:
        STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer -= 1
        # Safety net: when the timer expires, also clear the focus target
        # itself. _update_camera uses `target = mission2_camera_focus or
        # _player_pos()`, so an uncleared focus keeps the camera pinned
        # even after the timer hits 0. This is the root cause of the
        # "camera locks on the tanker after the blast" bug — the focus
        # was set in _explode_tanker but only the timer was decremented,
        # never the focus itself. The per-card close handlers (e.g.
        # blast_done) also clear this, but this catches any focus set
        # via _focus_camera_for_frames / inline that doesn't have a
        # matching close handler.
        if STATE.mission2_camera_focus_timer == 0:
            STATE.mission2_camera_focus = None

    if getattr(STATE, "mission2_heartbeat_active", False):
        STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer += 1
        if STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer % 72 == 0:
            audio.play("heartbeat", volume=0.18)
        # Point 12: Fade heartbeat as player takes first action (moves, shoots, etc.)
        # Also hard cap at ~12 seconds (720 frames)
        player_acting = False
        if STATE.player and not STATE.player_dead:
            keys = _last_keys
            if keys and (keys[pygame.K_w] or keys[pygame.K_s] or keys[pygame.K_a] or keys[pygame.K_d] or keys[pygame.K_e] or keys[pygame.K_SPACE] or keys[pygame.K_r] or keys[pygame.K_g] or keys[pygame.K_v]):
                player_acting = True
        if (STATE.mission2_stage == STAGE_COMBAT and (STATE.mission2_heartbeat_timer > 720 or player_acting)):
            STATE.mission2_heartbeat_active = False

    return _update_camera()


# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Drawing helpers
# ─────────────────────────────────────────────
# Mission 2 clue/prop draw tuning.  The same _draw_prop_icon() artwork is used
# both on the ground and inside the inspection cards; only the presentation shell
# changes.  Keep world props small, then use halos/markers for readability.
PROP_WORLD_SCALE = 0.62
PROP_CARD_SCALE = 1.05
PROP_WORLD_HALO_RADIUS = 22
MARKER_RADIUS = 22


def _world(pos, fcx, fcy):
    # Snap world-to-screen coordinates to whole pixels.  Mission 2 camera values
    # can be fractional because the player speed is fractional; drawing tiles and
    # clue icons at fractional positions causes shimmering/"glitchy" textures
    # while walking.
    return int(round(pos[0] - fcx)), int(round(pos[1] - fcy))

def _car_palette(world_rect):
    """Pick a deterministic car palette from the world rect center."""
    idx = (world_rect.centerx * 7 + world_rect.centery * 13) % len(_CIVILIAN_CAR_PALETTES)
    return _CIVILIAN_CAR_PALETTES[idx]


def _draw_civilian_car(surface, r, pal):
    """Top-down civilian sedan — hood on the RIGHT, trunk on the LEFT.

    Layout (faces EAST):
      [ TRUNK .... | CABIN (roof + glass) .... | HOOD ]
      4 wheels stick out top and bottom.
      Hood has a grille slit + 2 headlights at the front edge.
      Trunk has 2 taillights at the back edge + a license-plate strip.
      Cabin has a windshield (front), rear window (back), 2 side
      windows, and a roof panel with a subtle sunroof / door lines.
    """
    # --- Soft drop shadow (offset down-right) ---
    shad = pygame.Surface((r.w + 18, r.h + 14), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shad, (0, 0, 0, 95), shad.get_rect())
    surface.blit(shad, (r.x - 4, r.y + 5))

    body = pal["body"]
    body_dark = pal["body_dark"]
    body_light = pal["body_light"]
    roof = pal["roof"]
    glass = pal["glass"]
    glass_light = pal["glass_light"]
    trim = pal["trim"]

    # --- Body — main hull with rounded corners + a subtle top highlight
    # strip and a bottom shadow strip so it reads as a 3D shape. ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, body, r, border_radius=7)
    # Top highlight (1px lighter along the top edge)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, body_light,
                     (r.x, r.y, r.w, 3), border_radius=4)
    # Bottom shadow (2px darker along the bottom edge)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, body_dark,
                     (r.x, r.bottom - 4, r.w, 4), border_radius=4)
    # Side shadow strips — a thin darker line down each long side.
    pygame.draw.line(surface, body_dark,
                     (r.x + 1, r.y + 4), (r.x + 1, r.bottom - 5), 1)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, body_dark,
                     (r.right - 2, r.y + 4), (r.right - 2, r.bottom - 5), 1)

    # --- Layout: trunk (left ~22%), cabin (middle ~52%), hood (right ~26%) ---
    trunk_w = int(r.w * 0.22)
    hood_w = int(r.w * 0.26)
    cabin_x = r.x + trunk_w
    cabin_w = r.w - trunk_w - hood_w
    hood_x = r.x + r.w - hood_w

    # --- Cabin: roof panel + glass surround ---
    # Glass surround (slightly inset, darker than body) — reads as the
    # window opening around the roof.
    glass_rect = pygame.Rect(cabin_x + 2, r.y + 4, cabin_w - 4, r.h - 8)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, glass, glass_rect, border_radius=4)
    # Roof panel (centered inside the glass surround, leaves a glass border).
    roof_rect = pygame.Rect(cabin_x + 7, r.y + 7, cabin_w - 14, r.h - 14)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, roof, roof_rect, border_radius=3)
    # Roof highlight strip (1px lighter along the top of the roof).
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, body_light,
                     (roof_rect.x, roof_rect.y, roof_rect.w, 2), border_radius=2)
    # Sunroof seam — a thin darker line across the roof, slightly off-center.
    sunroof_x = roof_rect.x + roof_rect.w // 2 + 2
    pygame.draw.line(surface, body_dark,
                     (sunroof_x, roof_rect.y + 2),
                     (sunroof_x, roof_rect.bottom - 2), 1)
    # Windshield highlight — 1px light blue along the front edge of the
    # glass surround (sells reflection).
    pygame.draw.line(surface, glass_light,
                     (glass_rect.right - 1, glass_rect.y + 2),
                     (glass_rect.right - 1, glass_rect.bottom - 2), 1)
    # Rear-window highlight along the back edge.
    pygame.draw.line(surface, glass_light,
                     (glass_rect.x, glass_rect.y + 2),
                     (glass_rect.x, glass_rect.bottom - 2), 1)

    # --- Door lines — 2 vertical seams dividing the cabin into front
    # and rear doors. Slightly darker than the roof. ---
    door_x1 = cabin_x + cabin_w // 3
    door_x2 = cabin_x + (cabin_w * 2) // 3
    for dx in (door_x1, door_x2):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, body_dark,
                         (dx, r.y + 5), (dx, r.bottom - 5), 1)

    # --- Hood panel + front grille + headlights ---
    # Hood sits flush with the body; add a subtle seam where it meets
    # the cabin (the cowl line).
    pygame.draw.line(surface, body_dark,
                     (hood_x, r.y + 3), (hood_x, r.bottom - 3), 1)
    # Grille slit — a dark horizontal line near the front edge.
    grille_y = r.centery
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (20, 18, 16),
                     (hood_x + hood_w - 8, grille_y - 3, 6, 6), border_radius=1)
    # Headlights — 2 warm dots at the front corners.
    for hy in (r.y + 6, r.bottom - 8):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, pal["headlight"],
                           (r.right - 3, hy), 2)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (170, 150, 90),
                           (r.right - 3, hy), 3, 1)

    # --- Trunk panel + taillights + license plate ---
    # Seam where the trunk meets the cabin.
    pygame.draw.line(surface, body_dark,
                     (r.x + trunk_w, r.y + 3), (r.x + trunk_w, r.bottom - 3), 1)
    # Taillights — 2 red dots at the back corners.
    for ty in (r.y + 6, r.bottom - 8):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, pal["taillight"],
                           (r.x + 3, ty), 2)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (110, 28, 24),
                           (r.x + 3, ty), 3, 1)
    # License plate — a small lighter rect at the back edge center.
    plate = pygame.Rect(r.x - 1, r.centery - 2, 4, 5)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (180, 175, 160), plate)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (90, 88, 80), plate, 1)

    # --- Wheels (4 — 2 per side, sticking out top and bottom) ---
    # Axle positions along the car's length.
    axle_xs = [
        r.x + trunk_w + 8,         # rear axle (under trunk/cabin junction)
        hood_x + 6,                # front axle (under hood)
    ]
    wheel_r = 7
    for ax in axle_xs:
        # Top wheel (sticks up above the rect)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, pal["wheel"], (ax, r.y - 1), wheel_r)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, pal["wheel_rim"], (ax, r.y - 1), wheel_r, 1)
        # Hubcap
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (95, 90, 84), (ax, r.y - 1), 2)
        # Bottom wheel (sticks down below the rect)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, pal["wheel"], (ax, r.bottom + 1), wheel_r)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, pal["wheel_rim"], (ax, r.bottom + 1), wheel_r, 1)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (95, 90, 84), (ax, r.bottom + 1), 2)

    # --- Side mirrors — 2 tiny nubs sticking out from the cabin, just
    # behind the windshield. Small but they read as mirrors at this scale. ---
    for my in (r.y + 2, r.bottom - 4):
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, body_dark,
                         (cabin_x + cabin_w - 6, my, 3, 2))

    # --- Body outline (crisp dark border) ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, trim, r, 2, border_radius=7)


def _draw_burned_car(surface, r, pal, world_rect, bt=0):
    """Top-down burned-out civilian sedan.

    Same silhouette as _draw_civilian_car but blackened: blistered paint,
    blown-out glass, collapsed roof, melted wheels, scorch tail behind the
    car, and a faint persistent smolder in the engine bay + cabin. Seeded
    from the world rect so the damage pattern is stable frame-to-frame.
    """
    rng = random.Random(world_rect.centerx * 41 + world_rect.centery * 23)

    # --- Scorch tail — a wide dark patch under + behind the car (the
    # fire scorched the ground around it). Drawn first so the car sits
    # on top. ---
    scorch = pygame.Surface((r.w + 40, r.h + 28), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(scorch, (8, 6, 5, 170),
                        (0, 0, r.w + 40, r.h + 28))
    # Extra oily darker center.
    pygame.draw.ellipse(scorch, (4, 3, 3, 120),
                        (20, 8, r.w, r.h + 12))
    surface.blit(scorch, (r.x - 20, r.y - 8))

    # --- Charred palette — everything is blackened/ashy, with faint
    # hints of the original paint color showing through in patches. ---
    hull_black = (16, 13, 11)
    hull_dark = (26, 21, 18)
    hull_mid = (38, 32, 27)
    hull_light = (52, 44, 36)
    soot_col = (8, 6, 5)

    # --- Body — blackened hull. ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, hull_dark, r, border_radius=7)
    # Blistered paint patches — irregular lighter blotches where the
    # original paint bubbled + peeled, showing hints of the car's color.
    paint = pal["body"]
    for _ in range(5):
        bx = rng.randint(r.x + 4, r.right - 12)
        by = rng.randint(r.y + 4, r.bottom - 10)
        bw = rng.randint(10, 22)
        bh = rng.randint(6, 14)
        # Faint paint remnant — heavily darkened original color.
        col = (max(8, paint[0] // 3), max(6, paint[1] // 3), max(5, paint[2] // 3))
        patch = pygame.Surface((bw, bh), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(patch, (*col, 120), (0, 0, bw, bh))
        surface.blit(patch, (bx, by))
    # Heavy soot smudges across the whole body.
    soot = pygame.Surface((r.w, r.h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for _ in range(7):
        sx = rng.randint(0, max(1, r.w - 30))
        sy = rng.randint(0, max(1, r.h - 18))
        sw = rng.randint(16, 32)
        sh = rng.randint(10, 20)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(soot, (*soot_col, rng.randint(120, 180)),
                            (sx, sy, sw, sh))
    surface.blit(soot, (r.x, r.y))

    # --- Layout (same as the civilian car) ---
    trunk_w = int(r.w * 0.22)
    hood_w = int(r.w * 0.26)
    cabin_x = r.x + trunk_w
    cabin_w = r.w - trunk_w - hood_w
    hood_x = r.x + r.w - hood_w

    # --- Cabin: roof collapsed into the cabin — no glass, just a dark
    # cavity with charred framing. ---
    cabin_rect = pygame.Rect(cabin_x + 2, r.y + 4, cabin_w - 4, r.h - 8)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (5, 4, 3), cabin_rect, border_radius=3)
    # Collapsed roof ribs — a few twisted dark lines across the cavity.
    for _ in range(4):
        rx1 = rng.randint(cabin_rect.x + 3, cabin_rect.right - 3)
        ry1 = rng.randint(cabin_rect.y + 2, cabin_rect.bottom - 2)
        rx2 = rx1 + rng.randint(-10, 10)
        ry2 = ry1 + rng.randint(-6, 6)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_light, (rx1, ry1), (rx2, ry2), 2)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_mid, (rx1, ry1), (rx2, ry2), 1)

    # --- Door lines — still visible but charred. ---
    door_x1 = cabin_x + cabin_w // 3
    door_x2 = cabin_x + (cabin_w * 2) // 3
    for dx in (door_x1, door_x2):
        pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_black,
                         (dx, r.y + 5), (dx, r.bottom - 5), 1)

    # --- Hood seam + grille (melted). ---
    pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_black,
                     (hood_x, r.y + 3), (hood_x, r.bottom - 3), 1)
    # Melted grille — a darker blob.
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (4, 3, 3),
                     (hood_x + hood_w - 8, r.centery - 3, 6, 6), border_radius=2)

    # --- Trunk seam. ---
    pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_black,
                     (r.x + trunk_w, r.y + 3), (r.x + trunk_w, r.bottom - 3), 1)

    # --- Smoldering glow in the cabin + engine bay — fire still burning
    # inside. Slow pulse, seeded phase so it's independent of other cars. ---
    pulse = 0.55 + 0.45 * math.sin(bt * rng.uniform(0.08, 0.16) + rng.uniform(0, math.tau))
    # Cabin glow.
    cab_glow = pygame.Surface((cabin_w - 6, r.h - 12), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for gr, gc, ga in [(22, (255, 90, 25), int(90 * pulse)),
                       (14, (255, 140, 45), int(120 * pulse)),
                       (7, (255, 210, 110), int(150 * pulse))]:
        pygame.draw.circle(cab_glow, (*gc, ga),
                           (cab_glow.get_width() // 2, cab_glow.get_height() // 2), gr)
    surface.blit(cab_glow, (cabin_rect.x + 3, cabin_rect.y + 3))
    # Engine-bay glow (front).
    eng_glow = pygame.Surface((20, 16), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(eng_glow, (255, 100, 30, int(130 * pulse)), (0, 0, 20, 16))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(eng_glow, (255, 200, 90, int(90 * pulse)), (5, 3, 10, 10))
    surface.blit(eng_glow, (hood_x + 4, r.centery - 8))

    # --- Headlights / taillights — shattered, dark. ---
    for hy in (r.y + 6, r.bottom - 8):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (14, 11, 9), (r.right - 3, hy), 2)
    for ty in (r.y + 6, r.bottom - 8):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (40, 12, 10), (r.x + 3, ty), 2)

    # --- Wheels — melted / deflated. Drawn as dark blobs, smaller than
    # the civilian car's wheels, with no rim detail. ---
    axle_xs = [
        r.x + trunk_w + 8,
        hood_x + 6,
    ]
    for ax in axle_xs:
        # Melted top wheel — flattened ellipse.
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, hull_black,
                            (ax - 6, r.y - 4, 12, 7))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, hull_mid,
                            (ax - 6, r.y - 4, 12, 7), 1)
        # Melted bottom wheel.
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, hull_black,
                            (ax - 6, r.bottom - 3, 12, 7))
        pygame.draw.ellipse(surface, hull_mid,
                            (ax - 6, r.bottom - 3, 12, 7), 1)

    # --- Body outline — charred, slightly irregular. ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (6, 5, 4), r, 2, border_radius=7)


def _draw_car_burn_overlay(surface, r, world_rect, burn_progress):
    """Mid-burn overlay drawn ON TOP of the intact civilian car.

    As burn_progress climbs 0.0 → 1.0:
      - Soot patches grow across the body (alpha + count scale with bp).
      - The cabin glass darkens (smoke inside the cabin).
      - An escalating flame glow rises from the hood + cabin.
      - Flickering flame tongues lick up from the body.

    Seeded from the world rect so the soot pattern is stable frame-to-frame.
    Once burn_progress hits 1.0 the car flips to "burned" kind and this
    overlay is no longer used (the permanent _draw_burned_car takes over).
    """
    rng = random.Random(world_rect.centerx * 41 + world_rect.centery * 23 + 99)
    bp = max(0.0, min(1.0, burn_progress))
    ticks = pygame.time.get_ticks()

    # --- Soot overlay — grows with burn progress. ---
    soot_alpha = int(180 * bp)
    if soot_alpha > 10:
        soot = pygame.Surface((r.w, r.h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        patch_count = int(3 + bp * 5)
        for _ in range(patch_count):
            sx = rng.randint(2, max(3, r.w - 24))
            sy = rng.randint(2, max(3, r.h - 18))
            sw = rng.randint(14, 30)
            sh = rng.randint(10, 20)
            pygame.draw.ellipse(soot, (8, 6, 5, soot_alpha),
                                (sx, sy, sw, sh))
        surface.blit(soot, (r.x, r.y))

    # --- Darkening cabin glass — smoke fills the interior. ---
    if bp > 0.2:
        cabin_darken = int(140 * (bp - 0.2) / 0.8)
        # Approximate cabin rect (matches _draw_civilian_car layout).
        trunk_w = int(r.w * 0.22)
        hood_w = int(r.w * 0.26)
        cabin_x = r.x + trunk_w
        cabin_w = r.w - trunk_w - hood_w
        glass_rect = pygame.Rect(cabin_x + 2, r.y + 4, cabin_w - 4, r.h - 8)
        dark = pygame.Surface((glass_rect.w, glass_rect.h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        dark.fill((6, 5, 4, cabin_darken))
        surface.blit(dark, glass_rect.topleft)

    # --- Flame glow — rises from the hood + cabin, escalating with bp. ---
    glow_strength = bp  # 0 → 1
    if glow_strength > 0.05:
        pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * math.sin(ticks * 0.02)
        # Big soft glow under the car (light spill on the ground).
        ground_glow = pygame.Surface((r.w + 40, r.h + 20), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for gr, ga in [(r.h // 2 + 10, int(60 * glow_strength * pulse)),
                       (r.h // 4, int(90 * glow_strength * pulse)),
                       (8, int(120 * glow_strength * pulse))]:
            pygame.draw.ellipse(ground_glow, (255, 110, 30, ga),
                                (0, 0, r.w + 40, r.h + 20))
        surface.blit(ground_glow, (r.x - 20, r.y - 4))

        # Hot core glow on the body itself.
        core_glow = pygame.Surface((r.w, r.h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
        for gr, gc, ga in [
            (r.h // 2, (255, 90, 25), int(100 * glow_strength * pulse)),
            (r.h // 3, (255, 150, 45), int(130 * glow_strength * pulse)),
            (r.h // 5, (255, 220, 120), int(160 * glow_strength * pulse)),
        ]:
            pygame.draw.ellipse(core_glow, (*gc, ga),
                                (r.w // 2 - gr, r.h // 2 - gr // 2, gr * 2, gr))
        surface.blit(core_glow, r.topleft)

    # --- Flame tongues — flickering licks of fire rising from the body.
    # More + taller as the burn progresses. Seeded per-tongue so they
    # don't jump around frame-to-frame, but the flicker is animated. ---
    tongue_count = int(2 + bp * 5)
    for i in range(tongue_count):
        # Stable base position for this tongue.
        tx = r.x + rng.randint(8, max(10, r.w - 8))
        ty = r.y + rng.randint(4, max(6, r.h - 4))
        # Animated flicker height.
        h = int((10 + bp * 22) * (0.7 + 0.3 * math.sin(ticks * 0.03 + i * 1.7)))
        w = max(3, h // 3)
        # Outer orange flame.
        outer = [
            (tx, ty - h),
            (tx - w, ty + 3),
            (tx + w, ty + 3),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (255, 90, 20), outer)
        # Inner yellow-white core.
        inner = [
            (tx, ty - h * 0.6),
            (tx - w * 0.45, ty + 1),
            (tx + w * 0.45, ty + 1),
        ]
        pygame.draw.polygon(surface, (255, 210, 90), inner)


def _draw_vehicle(surface, rect, kind, fcx, fcy, vehicle=None):
    # If the tanker blast charred this vehicle, apply the push offset so
    # the wreck sits slightly off its original spot (reads as "blast
    # shoved it"). Set in _explode_tanker for civilian vehicles within
    # ~520px of the blast.
    off_x = off_y = 0
    if vehicle and vehicle.get("blast_charred"):
        off = vehicle.get("blast_offset", (0, 0))
        off_x, off_y = int(off[0]), int(off[1])
    r = pygame.Rect(rect.x - fcx + off_x, rect.y - fcy + off_y, rect.w, rect.h)

    if kind == "ipa_truck":
        # Route to the wrecked variant if the tanker blast charred it.
        # Pass the WORLD-space rect (not the screen-space `r`) so the
        # wreck geometry can be seeded from a stable position — otherwise
        # the fissures/soot jitter every frame as the camera moves.
        if vehicle and vehicle.get("blast_charred"):
            bt = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_blast_timer", 0)
            _draw_ipa_truck_charred(surface, r, bt, rect)
        else:
            _draw_ipa_truck(surface, r)
        return

    # --- Civilian cars + burned wrecks. Each car gets a deterministic
    # palette from its world rect center, so the same car always has the
    # same color. A car can become "burned" three ways:
    #   1. Tanker blast charring (blast_charred=True, kind flipped to
    #      "burned" in _explode_tanker).
    #   2. Molotov burning (burn_progress reaches 1.0, kind flipped to
    #      "burned" in _update_molotovs, molotov_burned=True).
    #   3. Mid-molotov-burn (burning=True, 0 < burn_progress < 1.0) —
    #      draws the intact car + an escalating charred overlay + flame
    #      glow so the player sees the car catch fire and burn.
    # All three flag conditions route to _draw_burned_car for the final
    # smoldering wreck variant; the mid-burn case is handled inline below. ---
    pal = _car_palette(rect)
    bt = getattr(STATE, "mission2_tanker_blast_timer", 0)
    bp = (vehicle.get("burn_progress", 0.0) if vehicle else 0.0)
    is_burning = bool(vehicle and vehicle.get("burning", False) and 0.0 < bp < 1.0)
    if kind == "burned" or (vehicle and vehicle.get("blast_charred")):
        _draw_burned_car(surface, r, pal, rect, bt)
    elif is_burning:
        # Mid-burn: draw the intact car, then overlay escalating char
        # + flame glow proportional to burn_progress.
        _draw_civilian_car(surface, r, pal)
        _draw_car_burn_overlay(surface, r, rect, bp)
    else:
        _draw_civilian_car(surface, r, pal)


def _draw_ipa_truck(surface, r):
    """Top-down IPA military cargo truck.

    Layout (truck faces EAST / right):
      [ CARGO CONTAINER .... | CAB ]
      6 wheels (3 axles) stick out top and bottom.
      The cargo container has panel lines, rivets, a tarp ridge, and a
      large IPA stencil. The cab has a windshield and roof.
    """
    # --- Shadow (soft offset ellipse) ---
    shad = pygame.Surface((r.w + 24, r.h + 20), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shad, (0, 0, 0, 90), shad.get_rect())
    surface.blit(shad, (r.x - 6, r.y + 4))

    # --- Colors ---
    cargo_dark  = (62, 48, 36)     # shadow side of cargo
    cargo_mid   = (78, 60, 44)     # cargo body
    cargo_light = (95, 74, 54)     # highlight side
    cab_col     = (68, 55, 42)     # cab roof
    cab_dark    = (50, 40, 30)
    windshield  = (18, 28, 38)     # dark glass
    wheel_col   = (12, 12, 14)
    wheel_rim   = (45, 42, 38)
    stencil_col = (200, 175, 130)  # cream stencil text
    rivet_col   = (45, 35, 26)

    # --- Cargo container (left ~68% of rect) ---
    cargo_w = int(r.w * 0.68)
    cargo_rect = pygame.Rect(r.x, r.y, cargo_w, r.h)
    # Base
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, cargo_mid, cargo_rect, border_radius=4)
    # Top highlight strip (1px lighter along the top edge)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, cargo_light, (cargo_rect.x, cargo_rect.y, cargo_rect.w, 3), border_radius=2)
    # Bottom shadow strip
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, cargo_dark, (cargo_rect.x, cargo_rect.bottom - 4, cargo_rect.w, 4), border_radius=2)
    # Tarp ridge line (center, vertical — reads as a folded tarp from above)
    ridge_x = cargo_rect.x + cargo_rect.w // 2
    pygame.draw.line(surface, cargo_dark, (ridge_x, cargo_rect.y + 3), (ridge_x, cargo_rect.bottom - 5), 2)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, cargo_light, (ridge_x + 1, cargo_rect.y + 3), (ridge_x + 1, cargo_rect.bottom - 5), 1)

    # Panel seams (2 horizontal lines dividing the cargo into 3rds)
    for frac in (0.33, 0.66):
        sy = cargo_rect.y + int(cargo_rect.h * frac)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, cargo_dark, (cargo_rect.x + 2, sy), (cargo_rect.right - 2, sy), 1)

    # Rivets along the seams (small dots)
    for frac in (0.33, 0.66):
        sy = cargo_rect.y + int(cargo_rect.h * frac)
        for rx in range(cargo_rect.x + 8, cargo_rect.right - 6, 16):
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, rivet_col, (rx, sy), 1)

    # IPA stencil (large, centered on cargo)
    stencil = font_terminal.render("IPA", True, stencil_col)
    # Scale up slightly for a painted-stencil look
    stencil = pygame.transform.scale(stencil, (stencil.get_width() + 6, stencil.get_height() + 4))
    surface.blit(stencil, (ridge_x - stencil.get_width() // 2, cargo_rect.y + 5))
    # Cargo number below the stencil
    num = font_terminal_small.render("CGO-12", True, (160, 140, 100))
    surface.blit(num, (ridge_x - num.get_width() // 2, cargo_rect.bottom - 16))

    # Tailgate line at the back (west/left edge)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, cargo_dark, (cargo_rect.x + 1, cargo_rect.y + 2), (cargo_rect.x + 1, cargo_rect.bottom - 2), 2)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, cargo_light, (cargo_rect.x + 3, cargo_rect.y + 2), (cargo_rect.x + 3, cargo_rect.bottom - 2), 1)

    # --- Cab (right ~32% of rect) ---
    cab_rect = pygame.Rect(r.x + cargo_w, r.y, r.w - cargo_w, r.h)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, cab_col, cab_rect, border_radius=4)
    # Cab shadow on the cargo-side edge
    pygame.draw.line(surface, cab_dark, (cab_rect.x, cab_rect.y), (cab_rect.x, cab_rect.bottom), 2)
    # Windshield (dark glass strip across the front of the cab)
    ws_rect = pygame.Rect(cab_rect.right - 18, cab_rect.y + 4, 12, cab_rect.h - 8)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, windshield, ws_rect, border_radius=2)
    # Windshield highlight (1px light blue along the top edge)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, (60, 80, 100), (ws_rect.x, ws_rect.y), (ws_rect.right, ws_rect.y), 1)
    # Roof highlight
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (85, 70, 52), (cab_rect.x + 2, cab_rect.y + 2, cab_rect.w - 22, 3), border_radius=2)

    # --- Wheels (6 wheels: 3 axles × 2 sides) ---
    # Axle positions along the truck's length
    axle_xs = [
        cargo_rect.x + 16,                    # rear axle
        cargo_rect.x + cargo_w // 2,          # mid axle
        cab_rect.right - 14,                   # front axle (under cab)
    ]
    wheel_r = 7
    for ax in axle_xs:
        # Top wheel (sticks up above the rect)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, wheel_col, (ax, r.y - 1), wheel_r)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, wheel_rim, (ax, r.y - 1), wheel_r, 1)
        # Bottom wheel (sticks down below the rect)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, wheel_col, (ax, r.bottom + 1), wheel_r)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, wheel_rim, (ax, r.bottom + 1), wheel_r, 1)

    # --- Headlights (2 small warm dots at the front/east edge of cab) ---
    for hy in (cab_rect.y + 8, cab_rect.bottom - 10):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (230, 200, 120), (cab_rect.right - 3, hy), 2)
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (180, 150, 80), (cab_rect.right - 3, hy), 3, 1)

    # --- Outline (crisp dark border around the whole truck) ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (30, 22, 16), r, 2, border_radius=4)


def _draw_ipa_truck_charred(surface, r, bt, world_rect):
    """Wrecked IPA truck — drawn after the tanker blast chars it.

    The truck is ~246px from the tanker, so it takes the full force of
    the blast: blackened cargo container, buckled/twisted panels, a
    blown-out windshield with glowing cracks, missing/burned-off tarp,
    scorched wheels, and a persistent smoldering glow in the engine bay
    + cargo hold. The silhouette stays recognizable as the same truck
    the player inspected earlier, just destroyed.

    ``world_rect`` is the truck's WORLD-space rect (not screen-space).
    All wreck geometry is seeded from it so the fissures/soot/cracks
    stay locked to the truck as the camera moves — previously the seed
    used the screen-space rect (``r``), which changed every frame as
    the player walked, making the fissures jitter wildly.
    """
    # --- ONE stable RNG, seeded from the world-space rect center.
    # This never changes as the camera moves, so all the wreck geometry
    # (fissure paths, soot smudges, windshield cracks, wheel presence)
    # is stable frame-to-frame. ---
    rng = random.Random(world_rect.centerx * 31 + world_rect.centery * 17)

    # --- Shadow (darker, wider — the wreck sits heavier) ---
    shad = pygame.Surface((r.w + 30, r.h + 24), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(shad, (0, 0, 0, 140), shad.get_rect())
    surface.blit(shad, (r.x - 8, r.y + 6))

    # --- Charred color palette — everything is blackened/ashy. ---
    hull_black   = (18, 14, 11)
    hull_dark    = (28, 22, 18)
    hull_mid     = (42, 34, 28)
    hull_light   = (58, 48, 38)
    soot_col     = (10, 8, 7)
    glow_col     = (255, 100, 30)
    glow_core    = (255, 200, 90)
    crack_col    = (255, 80, 25)

    cargo_w = int(r.w * 0.68)
    cargo_rect = pygame.Rect(r.x, r.y, cargo_w, r.h)
    cab_rect = pygame.Rect(r.x + cargo_w, r.y, r.w - cargo_w, r.h)

    # --- Cargo container — blackened, buckled. ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, hull_dark, cargo_rect, border_radius=4)
    # Buckled roof — irregular darker patches where the metal caved in.
    for bx, by, bw, bh in [
        (cargo_rect.x + 6, cargo_rect.y + 3, cargo_w // 3, r.h - 8),
        (cargo_rect.x + cargo_w // 2 - 10, cargo_rect.y + 2, 28, r.h - 6),
        (cargo_rect.right - cargo_w // 3, cargo_rect.y + 4, cargo_w // 4, r.h - 10),
    ]:
        pygame.draw.rect(surface, hull_black, (bx, by, bw, bh), border_radius=3)
    # Heavy soot smudges across the whole cargo. Pre-computed from the
    # stable rng so they don't jitter.
    soot = pygame.Surface((cargo_w, r.h), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for _ in range(6):
        sx = rng.randint(0, max(1, cargo_w - 36))
        sy = rng.randint(0, max(1, r.h - 26))
        sw = rng.randint(18, 36)
        sh = rng.randint(14, 26)
        sa = rng.randint(100, 160)
        pygame.draw.ellipse(soot, (*soot_col, sa), (sx, sy, sw, sh))
    surface.blit(soot, (cargo_rect.x, cargo_rect.y))

    # Tarp ridge — burned away, just a twisted metal rib left. Offset
    # is stable from the rng.
    ridge_x = cargo_rect.x + cargo_w // 2
    ridge_offset = rng.randint(-3, 3)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_black, (ridge_x, cargo_rect.y + 3),
                     (ridge_x + ridge_offset, cargo_rect.bottom - 5), 3)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_light, (ridge_x + 1, cargo_rect.y + 3),
                     (ridge_x + 1, cargo_rect.bottom - 5), 1)

    # Panel seams — still visible but charred.
    for frac in (0.33, 0.66):
        sy = cargo_rect.y + int(cargo_rect.h * frac)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_black,
                         (cargo_rect.x + 2, sy), (cargo_rect.right - 2, sy), 1)

    # IPA stencil — scorched/peeling. Render faintly so it's barely
    # readable, sells that the truck is the same one from before.
    stencil = font_terminal.render("IPA", True, (70, 58, 42))
    stencil = pygame.transform.scale(stencil, (stencil.get_width() + 6, stencil.get_height() + 4))
    stencil.set_alpha(120)
    surface.blit(stencil, (ridge_x - stencil.get_width() // 2, cargo_rect.y + 5))

    # --- Pre-compute fissure geometry ONCE from the stable rng, then
    # draw with a per-fissure pulse so they flicker independently instead
    # of all brightening/dimming in lockstep (which looked mechanical).
    # Each fissure gets its own phase offset + slightly different pulse
    # speed so the overall glow reads as natural smoldering, not a
    # synchronized strobe. ---
    fissures = []
    for _ in range(6):
        fx = rng.randint(cargo_rect.x + 6, cargo_rect.right - 6)
        fy = rng.randint(cargo_rect.y + 4, cargo_rect.bottom - 4)
        segs = rng.randint(2, 4)
        pts = [(fx, fy)]
        for _seg in range(segs):
            pts.append((pts[-1][0] + rng.randint(-18, 18),
                        pts[-1][1] + rng.randint(-10, 10)))
        fissures.append({
            "pts": pts,
            "phase": rng.uniform(0, math.tau),   # independent flicker phase
            "speed": rng.uniform(0.10, 0.22),    # independent flicker speed
        })
    for fis in fissures:
        # Per-fissure pulse — each crack glows on its own rhythm.
        p = 0.55 + 0.45 * math.sin(bt * fis["speed"] + fis["phase"])
        pts = fis["pts"]
        for seg in range(len(pts) - 1):
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (*crack_col, int(170 * p)),
                             pts[seg], pts[seg + 1], 3)
            pygame.draw.line(surface, (*glow_core, int(140 * p)),
                             pts[seg], pts[seg + 1], 1)

    # --- Smoldering glow in the cargo hold — fire still burning inside.
    # Slow overall pulse, independent of the fissure flicker. ---
    cargo_pulse = 0.7 + 0.3 * math.sin(bt * 0.08)
    cargo_glow = pygame.Surface((cargo_w - 20, r.h - 16), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    for gr, gc, ga in [(40, (255, 90, 25), int(80 * cargo_pulse)),
                       (24, (255, 150, 45), int(110 * cargo_pulse)),
                       (12, (255, 220, 120), int(140 * cargo_pulse))]:
        pygame.draw.circle(cargo_glow, (*gc, ga),
                           (cargo_glow.get_width() // 2, cargo_glow.get_height() // 2), gr)
    surface.blit(cargo_glow, (cargo_rect.x + 10, cargo_rect.y + 8))

    # --- Cab — blackened, windshield blown out. ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, hull_dark, cab_rect, border_radius=4)
    pygame.draw.line(surface, hull_black,
                     (cab_rect.x, cab_rect.y), (cab_rect.x, cab_rect.bottom), 2)
    # Blown-out windshield — dark hole with glowing crack edges.
    ws_rect = pygame.Rect(cab_rect.right - 18, cab_rect.y + 4, 12, cab_rect.h - 8)
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (5, 4, 3), ws_rect, border_radius=2)
    # Cracks radiating from the windshield impact. Pre-computed from the
    # stable rng.
    ws_pulse = 0.6 + 0.4 * math.sin(bt * 0.18 + 1.5)
    for _ in range(5):
        x1 = ws_rect.x + rng.randint(2, max(3, ws_rect.w - 2))
        y1 = ws_rect.y + rng.randint(2, max(3, ws_rect.h - 2))
        x2 = x1 + rng.randint(-10, 10)
        y2 = y1 + rng.randint(-10, 10)
        pygame.draw.line(surface, (*crack_col, int(150 * ws_pulse)), (x1, y1), (x2, y2), 1)

    # --- Engine-bay smolder — persistent glow at the front of the cab. ---
    eng_pulse = 0.65 + 0.35 * math.sin(bt * 0.12 + 2.3)
    eng_glow = pygame.Surface((24, 20), pygame.SRCALPHA)
    pygame.draw.ellipse(eng_glow, (*glow_col, int(140 * eng_pulse)), (0, 0, 24, 20))
    pygame.draw.ellipse(eng_glow, (*glow_core, int(100 * eng_pulse)), (6, 4, 12, 12))
    surface.blit(eng_glow, (cab_rect.right - 22, cab_rect.centery - 10))

    # --- Wheels — scorched, one or two missing (just stubs). Which
    # wheels survived is pre-computed from the stable rng so it doesn't
    # change every frame. ---
    axle_xs = [
        cargo_rect.x + 16,
        cargo_rect.x + cargo_w // 2,
        cab_rect.right - 14,
    ]
    for i, ax in enumerate(axle_xs):
        top_alive = rng.random() > 0.25
        bot_alive = rng.random() > 0.25
        if top_alive:
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (8, 8, 10), (ax, r.y - 1), 6)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (30, 28, 26), (ax, r.y - 1), 6, 1)
        else:
            # Burned-off stub.
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, hull_black, (ax, r.y - 1), 3)
        if bot_alive:
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (8, 8, 10), (ax, r.bottom + 1), 6)
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, (30, 28, 26), (ax, r.bottom + 1), 6, 1)
        else:
            pygame.draw.circle(surface, hull_black, (ax, r.bottom + 1), 3)

    # --- Headlights — shattered, one still faintly glowing. ---
    for hy in (cab_rect.y + 8, cab_rect.bottom - 10):
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (20, 16, 12), (cab_rect.right - 3, hy), 2)
    # One surviving headlight — flickering.
    if bt % 60 < 30:
        pygame.draw.circle(surface, (255, 200, 100),
                           (cab_rect.right - 3, cab_rect.y + 8), 2)

    # --- Outline — charred, irregular. ---
    pygame.draw.rect(surface, (8, 6, 5), r, 2, border_radius=4)
